===== src/api/base44Client.js =====
import { createClient } from "@base44/sdk";
import { getAppParams } from "@/lib/app-params";

const { appId, token, functionsVersion, appBaseUrl } = getAppParams();

export const base44 = createClient({ appId, token: token ?? undefined, functionsVersion, serverUrl: "", appBaseUrl });


===== src/components/DefaultCatchBoundary.jsx =====
import { ErrorComponent, Link, rootRouteId, useMatch, useRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export function DefaultCatchBoundary({ error }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const isRoot = useMatch({ strict: false, select: (state) => state.id === rootRouteId });

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-2xl flex-col items-center gap-4 py-24">
      <ErrorComponent error={error} />
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => router.invalidate()}>
          Try again
        </Button>
        <Button asChild>{isRoot ? <Link to="/">Home</Link> : <Link to="/" onClick={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); window.history.back(); }}>Go back</Link>}</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== src/components/NotFound.jsx =====
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";

export function NotFound({ children }) {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-lg flex-col items-center gap-4 py-24 text-center">
      <p className="text-6xl font-semibold tracking-tight">404</p>
      <p className="text-muted-foreground">{children ?? "The page you are looking for does not exist."}</p>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => window.history.back()}>
          Go back
        </Button>
        <Button asChild>
          <Link to="/">Start over</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== src/components/app/AdminGuard.jsx =====
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Lock } from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "@/lib/AuthContext";

export default function AdminGuard({ children }) {
  const { user, isLoadingAuth } = useAuth();

  if (isLoadingAuth) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[1500px] px-5 py-10 sm:px-8">
        <div className="h-72 animate-pulse rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (user?.role !== "admin") {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[620px] px-5 py-16 sm:px-8">
        <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card px-6 py-8">
          <Lock className="h-4 w-4 text-sea-mid" />
          <h1 className="mt-4 font-heading text-[1.35rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">
            Área administrativa
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-[0.84rem] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
            Esta área é restrita à equipe Payonda. Sua conta de cliente acompanha a operação pelo painel
            da conta.
          </p>
          <Link
            to="/painel"
            className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center rounded-[2px] border border-primary bg-primary px-4 py-2 font-mono text-[0.58rem] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/88"
          >
            Ir para o painel do cliente
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return children;
}

===== src/components/app/AppBar.jsx =====
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import {
  ArrowRightLeft,
  Building2,
  Code2,
  LayoutDashboard,
  QrCode,
  Receipt,
  ShieldCheck,
  Store,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "@/lib/AuthContext";

const clienteTabs = [
  { id: "painel", to: "/painel", label: "Visão geral", Icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { id: "cobrancas", to: "/cobrancas", label: "Receber", Icon: QrCode },
  { id: "extrato", to: "/extrato", label: "Extrato", Icon: Receipt },
];

const adminTabs = [
  { id: "admin", to: "/admin", label: "Administrativo", Icon: ShieldCheck },
  { id: "fluxo", to: "/fluxo", label: "Fluxo", Icon: ArrowRightLeft },
  { id: "empresas", to: "/empresas", label: "Empresas", Icon: Building2 },
  { id: "equipe", to: "/equipe", label: "Equipe", Icon: Users },
  { id: "ativos", to: "/ativos", label: "Ativos", Icon: Store },
  { id: "codigo", to: "/codigo", label: "Código", Icon: Code2 },
];

function Tab({ tab, active }) {
  const isActive = tab.id === active;
  return (
    <Link
      to={tab.to}
      className={`-mb-px flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 px-3 font-mono text-[0.58rem] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] transition-colors ${
        isActive
          ? "border-foreground text-foreground"
          : "border-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
      }`}
    >
      <tab.Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
      <span className="py-3.5">{tab.label}</span>
    </Link>
  );
}

export default function AppBar({ active, right }) {
  const { user, isLoadingAuth } = useAuth();
  const isAdmin = !isLoadingAuth && user?.role === "admin";

  return (
    <div className="border-b border-border bg-background">
      <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-[1500px] items-stretch justify-between gap-4 px-5 sm:px-8">
        <div className="flex items-stretch overflow-x-auto">
          {clienteTabs.map((tab) => (
            <Tab key={tab.id} tab={tab} active={active} />
          ))}

          {isAdmin && (
            <>
              <span className="mx-2 my-auto hidden h-3.5 w-px bg-border sm:block" />
              {adminTabs.map((tab) => (
                <Tab key={tab.id} tab={tab} active={active} />
              ))}
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="label-mono hidden sm:inline">
            {isAdmin ? "interno · equipe Payonda" : "conta PJ · nov 2026"}
          </span>
          {right}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/BoardColumn.jsx =====
import { Droppable } from "@hello-pangea/dnd";
import EmpresaCard from "@/components/app/EmpresaCard";
import { compactBRL } from "@/lib/app-data";

export default function BoardColumn({ etapa, empresas, onDelete }) {
  const volume = empresas.reduce((soma, e) => soma + (e.volume_mensal ?? 0), 0);

  return (
    <div className="flex w-[292px] shrink-0 flex-col lg:w-auto">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between border-b border-white/[0.09] pb-2.5">
        <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
          <h2 className="font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-foreground">
            {etapa.label}
          </h2>
          <span className="font-mono text-[0.58rem] text-muted-foreground">{empresas.length}</span>
        </div>
        <span className="font-mono text-[0.58rem] text-muted-foreground">{compactBRL(volume)}</span>
      </div>

      <Droppable droppableId={etapa.id}>
        {(provided, snapshot) => (
          <div
            ref={provided.innerRef}
            {...provided.droppableProps}
            className={`mt-2 flex min-h-[180px] flex-1 flex-col gap-2 rounded-md transition-colors ${
              snapshot.isDraggingOver ? "bg-primary/[0.05]" : ""
            }`}
          >
            {empresas.map((empresa, index) => (
              <EmpresaCard
                key={empresa.id}
                empresa={empresa}
                index={index}
                onDelete={onDelete}
              />
            ))}
            {provided.placeholder}
            {empresas.length === 0 && !snapshot.isDraggingOver && (
              <p className="px-1 py-6 text-[0.7rem] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground/60">
                {etapa.nota}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </Droppable>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/CustoPorMeio.jsx =====
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { pct, brl, MEIO_LABEL } from "@/lib/app-data";

export default function CustoPorMeio({ rows, volume }) {
  const maior = Math.max(...rows.map((r) => r.taxa), 1);

  return (
    <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between border-b border-border px-5 py-4">
        <h2 className="font-heading text-[0.95rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">Custo por meio</h2>
        <span className="label-mono">clique para filtrar</span>
      </div>

      <div className="divide-y divide-border">
        {rows.map((row) => {
          const share = volume ? (row.valor / volume) * 100 : 0;
          return (
            <Link
              key={row.meio}
              to="/extrato"
              search={{ meio: row.meio }}
              className="block px-5 py-4 transition-colors hover:bg-white/[0.03]"
            >
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4">
                <span className="text-[0.86rem] text-foreground/85">{MEIO_LABEL[row.meio] ?? row.meio}</span>
                <span className="whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[0.92rem] text-foreground">
                  {brl(row.taxa, 2)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="h-1.5 flex-1 overflow-hidden bg-white/[0.07]">
                  <div
                    className="h-full bg-sea-mid"
                    style={{ width: `${Math.max((row.taxa / maior) * 100, 3)}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
                <span className="w-24 shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap text-right font-mono text-[0.62rem] text-muted-foreground">
                  {pct(share, 0)} do volume
                </span>
              </div>
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/EmpresaCard.jsx =====
import { Draggable } from "@hello-pangea/dnd";
import { Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { brl, compactBRL, SEGMENTO_LABEL, MEIO_LABEL, ECONOMIA_RATE } from "@/lib/app-data";

export default function EmpresaCard({ empresa, index, onDelete }) {
  const economia = (empresa.volume_mensal ?? 0) * ECONOMIA_RATE;

  return (
    <Draggable draggableId={empresa.id} index={index}>
      {(provided, snapshot) => (
        <div
          ref={provided.innerRef}
          {...provided.draggableProps}
          {...provided.dragHandleProps}
          className={`group rounded-md border bg-card px-3.5 py-3 transition-colors ${
            snapshot.isDragging
              ? "border-foreground/50 bg-card shadow-[0_16px_32px_-20px_rgba(0,0,0,0.9)]"
              : "border-border hover:border-foreground/25"
          }`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
            <p className="text-[0.82rem] font-medium leading-snug text-foreground">{empresa.nome}</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onDelete(empresa)}
              aria-label={`Remover ${empresa.nome}`}
              className="shrink-0 rounded p-1 text-muted-foreground/40 opacity-0 transition-opacity hover:bg-white/[0.06] hover:text-destructive group-hover:opacity-100"
            >
              <Trash2 className="h-3 w-3" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="rounded-sm border border-white/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[0.54rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
              {SEGMENTO_LABEL[empresa.segmento] ?? empresa.segmento}
            </span>
            <span className="font-mono text-[0.54rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground/70">
              {MEIO_LABEL[empresa.meio_principal] ?? empresa.meio_principal}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-3 flex items-end justify-between border-t border-white/[0.06] pt-2.5">
            <div>
              <p className="font-mono text-[0.54rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">volume/mês</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.76rem] text-foreground">
                {compactBRL(empresa.volume_mensal)}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
              <p className="font-mono text-[0.54rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">economia</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.76rem] text-primary">{brl(economia)}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Draggable>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/EmpresaDialog.jsx =====
import { useState } from "react";
import { Plus } from "lucide-react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { SEGMENTOS, MEIOS } from "@/lib/app-data";

const vazio = {
  nome: "",
  cnpj: "",
  segmento: "ecommerce",
  meio_principal: "misto",
  volume_mensal: "",
  contato: "",
  notas: "",
};

const campo =
  "w-full rounded-md border border-white/12 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-[0.85rem] text-foreground outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-muted-foreground/60 focus:border-primary/60";

const rotulo = "mb-1.5 block font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-muted-foreground";

export default function EmpresaDialog({ onCreated }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(vazio);
  const [salvando, setSalvando] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState("");

  const set = (key) => (event) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [key]: event.target.value }));

  async function salvar(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!form.nome.trim()) {
      setErro("Informe o nome da empresa.");
      return;
    }
    setSalvando(true);
    setErro("");
    try {
      const criada = await base44.entities.Empresa.create({
        nome: form.nome.trim(),
        cnpj: form.cnpj.trim(),
        segmento: form.segmento,
        meio_principal: form.meio_principal,
        volume_mensal: Number(String(form.volume_mensal).replace(/\D/g, "")) || 0,
        contato: form.contato.trim(),
        notas: form.notas.trim(),
        etapa: "auditoria",
      });
      onCreated(criada);
      setForm(vazio);
      setOpen(false);
    } catch (e) {
      setErro(e?.message ?? "Não foi possível salvar a empresa.");
    } finally {
      setSalvando(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <DialogTrigger asChild>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-primary px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-primary-foreground transition-all hover:brightness-110"
        >
          <Plus className="h-3 w-3" /> Nova empresa
        </button>
      </DialogTrigger>

      <DialogContent className="max-w-lg border-white/10 bg-card p-6">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="font-heading text-[1.05rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">
            Cadastrar empresa
          </DialogTitle>
        </DialogHeader>

        <form onSubmit={salvar} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className={rotulo} htmlFor="nome">Razão social</label>
            <input id="nome" className={campo} value={form.nome} onChange={set("nome")} placeholder="Onda Comércio LTDA" />
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label className={rotulo} htmlFor="cnpj">CNPJ</label>
              <input id="cnpj" className={campo} value={form.cnpj} onChange={set("cnpj")} placeholder="00.000.000/0001-00" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={rotulo} htmlFor="volume">Volume mensal (R$)</label>
              <input id="volume" inputMode="numeric" className={campo} value={form.volume_mensal} onChange={set("volume_mensal")} placeholder="250000" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label className={rotulo} htmlFor="segmento">Segmento</label>
              <select id="segmento" className={campo} value={form.segmento} onChange={set("segmento")}>
                {SEGMENTOS.map((s) => (
                  <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className={rotulo} htmlFor="meio">Meio principal</label>
              <select id="meio" className={campo} value={form.meio_principal} onChange={set("meio_principal")}>
                {MEIOS.map((m) => (
                  <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.label}</option>
                ))}
                <option value="misto">Misto</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className={rotulo} htmlFor="contato">Contato</label>
            <input id="contato" className={campo} value={form.contato} onChange={set("contato")} placeholder="Nome · e-mail" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className={rotulo} htmlFor="notas">Notas da auditoria</label>
            <textarea id="notas" rows={3} className={campo} value={form.notas} onChange={set("notas")} placeholder="Extrato recebido, custo atual 2,4%..." />
          </div>

          {erro && <p className="text-[0.78rem] text-destructive">{erro}</p>}

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
              className="rounded-md border border-white/12 px-4 py-2 font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={salvando}
              className="rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-primary-foreground transition-all hover:brightness-110 disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {salvando ? "Salvando…" : "Cadastrar"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/EscudoResumo.jsx =====
import { ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { brl } from "@/lib/app-data";

const controles = [
  "Validação de CNPJ e boleto antes de pagar",
  "Autenticação 3DS no checkout",

            {controle}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/Kpi.jsx =====
export default function Kpi({ label, value, note, tone = "neutral" }) {
  const toneClass =
    tone === "positive" ? "text-positive" : tone === "warn" ? "text-secondary" : "text-foreground";

  return (
    <div className="px-5 py-5">
      <p className="label-mono">{label}</p>
      <p
        className={`mt-2.5 whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[1.5rem] leading-none tracking-[-0.03em] sm:text-[1.75rem] ${toneClass}`}
      >
        {value}
      </p>
      {note && <p className="mt-2 text-[0.72rem] leading-snug text-muted-foreground">{note}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/Lancamentos.jsx =====
import { brl, dataCurta, MEIO_LABEL } from "@/lib/app-data";

const filtros = [
  { id: "todos", label: "Todos" },
  { id: "pix", label: "Pix" },
  { id: "cartao", label: "Cartão" },
  { id: "boleto", label: "Boleto" },
  { id: "cambio", label: "Câmbio" },
];

const statusTone = {
  aprovado: "text-positive",
  pendente: "text-secondary",
  recusado: "text-destructive",
};

export default function Lancamentos({ items, filtro, onFiltro }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-border px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <h2 className="font-heading text-[0.95rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">Lançamentos</h2>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
          {filtros.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onFiltro(f.id)}
              className={`rounded-[2px] border px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] transition-colors ${
                filtro === f.id
                  ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground"
                  : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-foreground/40 hover:text-foreground"
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full min-w-[620px] border-collapse text-left">
          <thead>
            <tr className="font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
              <th className="px-5 py-3 font-normal">Data</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 font-normal">Descrição</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 font-normal">Meio</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 text-right font-normal">Valor</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 text-right font-normal">Tarifa</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 text-right font-normal">Status</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {items.map((item) => {
              const saida = item.natureza === "saida";
              return (
                <tr key={item.id} className="border-t border-border">
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 font-mono text-[0.7rem] text-muted-foreground">
                    {dataCurta(item.data)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-[0.8rem] text-foreground/85">{item.descricao}</td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 font-mono text-[0.64rem] uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-muted-foreground">
                    {MEIO_LABEL[item.meio] ?? item.meio}
                  </td>
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-right font-mono text-[0.78rem] text-foreground">
                    {saida ? "−" : "+"}
                    {brl(item.valor, 2)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-5 py-3 text-right font-mono text-[0.72rem] text-muted-foreground">
                    {item.taxa ? brl(item.taxa, 2) : "—"}
                  </td>
                  <td
                    className={`px-5 py-3 text-right font-mono text-[0.58rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] ${
                      statusTone[item.status] ?? "text-muted-foreground"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {item.status}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
            {items.length === 0 && (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={6} className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-[0.8rem] text-muted-foreground">
                  Nenhum lançamento neste meio.
                </td>
              </tr>
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/VolumeChart.jsx =====
import { Area, AreaChart, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis } from "recharts";
import { compactBRL, dataCurta } from "@/lib/app-data";

export default function VolumeChart({ serie }) {
  const dados = (serie ?? []).map((ponto) => ({ ...ponto, label: dataCurta(ponto.data) }));

  return (
    <div className="h-44 w-full sm:h-52">
      <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
        <AreaChart data={dados} margin={{ top: 10, right: 0, bottom: 0, left: 0 }}>
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id="conta-sea-fill" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="0%" stopColor="hsl(var(--sea-mid))" stopOpacity={0.45} />
              <stop offset="100%" stopColor="hsl(var(--sea-deep))" stopOpacity={0} />
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>
          <XAxis
            dataKey="label"
            tickLine={false}
            axisLine={false}
            interval="preserveStartEnd"
            tick={{ fill: "hsl(var(--muted-foreground))", fontSize: 10, fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)" }}
          />
          <Tooltip
            cursor={{ stroke: "hsl(var(--border))" }}
            contentStyle={{
              background: "hsl(var(--card))",
              border: "1px solid hsl(var(--border))",
              borderRadius: 2,
              fontFamily: "var(--font-mono)",
              fontSize: 11,
              color: "hsl(var(--foreground))",
            }}
            labelStyle={{ color: "hsl(var(--muted-foreground))", fontSize: 10 }}
            formatter={(valor) => [compactBRL(valor), "volume"]}
          />
          <Area
            type="monotone"
            dataKey="valor"
            stroke="hsl(var(--sea-light))"
            strokeWidth={1.5}
            fill="url(#conta-sea-fill)"
          />
        </AreaChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </div>
  );
}

import { Search, X } from "lucide-react";

export const FILTROS_ADMIN = [
  { id: "tudo", label: "Tudo" },
  { id: "empresas", label: "Empresas" },
  { id: "lancamentos", label: "Lançamentos" },

export default function BuscaAdmin({ termo, onTermo, escopo, onEscopo }) {
  return (
    <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card px-4 py-3 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">

        <Search className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground" />
        <input
          value={termo}
          onChange={(evento) => onTermo(evento.target.value)}
          placeholder="Buscar empresa, lançamento ou valor (ex: 4.500)"

          </button>
        ))}

}

===== src/components/app/admin/CarteiraTabela.jsx =====
import { brl, compactBRL, ETAPAS, SEGMENTO_LABEL, TAXA_PAYONDA } from "@/lib/app-data";

const ETAPA_LABEL = Object.fromEntries(ETAPAS.map((etapa) => [etapa.id, etapa.label]));

export default function CarteiraTabela({ empresas }) {
  const lista = [...empresas].sort((a, b) => (b.volume_mensal ?? 0) - (a.volume_mensal ?? 0));

  return (
    <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between border-b border-border px-5 py-4">
        <h2 className="font-heading text-[0.95rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">Carteira de empresas</h2>
        <span className="font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground">
          {lista.length} na base
        </span>
      </div>

      {lista.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="px-5 py-8 text-[0.8rem] text-muted-foreground">
          Nenhuma empresa cadastrada — use “Nova empresa” para iniciar uma auditoria.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
          {lista.map((empresa) => (
            <li key={empresa.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-3.5">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-[0.86rem] text-foreground">{empresa.nome}</p>
                <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                  {SEGMENTO_LABEL[empresa.segmento] ?? empresa.segmento} ·{" "}
                  {ETAPA_LABEL[empresa.etapa] ?? "Auditoria"}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-5 text-right sm:gap-8">
                <div>
                  <p className="font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">volume/mês</p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[0.8rem] text-foreground">
                    {compactBRL(empresa.volume_mensal)}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="hidden sm:block">
                  <p className="font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">receita</p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[0.8rem] text-sea-light">
                    {brl((empresa.volume_mensal ?? 0) * TAXA_PAYONDA)}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/admin/FluxoPorCliente.jsx =====
import { brl } from "@/lib/app-data";

/** Fecha por cliente: quanto cada conta gerou de volume, tarifa e líquido. */
export default function FluxoPorCliente({ itens }) {
  const mapa = new Map();

  itens
    .filter((item) => item.status === "pago")
    .forEach((item) => {
      const atual = mapa.get(item.cliente) ?? { cobrancas: 0, volume: 0, tarifa: 0, liquido: 0 };
      mapa.set(item.cliente, {
        cobrancas: atual.cobrancas + 1,
        volume: atual.volume + item.valor,
        tarifa: atual.tarifa + item.taxa,
        liquido: atual.liquido + item.liquido,
      });
    });

  const linhas = [...mapa.entries()]
    .map(([cliente, dados]) => ({ cliente, ...dados }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.tarifa - a.tarifa);

  return (
    <div className="mt-4 rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="border-b border-border px-5 py-4">
        <h2 className="font-heading text-[0.95rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">Por cliente</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[0.74rem] text-muted-foreground">
          Receita da Payonda e valor creditado em cada conta.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="divide-y divide-border">
        {linhas.map((linha) => (
          <div
            key={linha.cliente}
            className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-x-6 gap-y-1 px-5 py-3"
          >
            <div className="min-w-[180px]">
              <p className="text-[0.8rem] text-foreground/90">{linha.cliente}</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.58rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                {linha.cobrancas} {linha.cobrancas === 1 ? "cobrança paga" : "cobranças pagas"} ·{" "}
                {brl(linha.volume, 2)}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-baseline gap-6">
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="label-mono">tarifa</p>
                <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[0.78rem] text-secondary">{brl(linha.tarifa, 2)}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="label-mono">líquido</p>
                <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[0.78rem] text-positive">{brl(linha.liquido, 2)}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}

        {linhas.length === 0 && (
          <p className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-[0.8rem] text-muted-foreground">
            Nenhuma cobrança paga ainda.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/admin/FunilEtapas.jsx =====
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { ETAPAS, compactBRL } from "@/lib/app-data";

export default function FunilEtapas({ porEtapa }) {
  const maior = Math.max(1, ...ETAPAS.map((etapa) => porEtapa[etapa.id]?.volume ?? 0));

  return (
    <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border px-5 py-4">
        <h2 className="font-heading text-[0.95rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">Funil de migração</h2>
        <Link
          to="/empresas"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
        >
          Abrir board
          <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
        </Link>
      </div>

      <ul className="divide-y divide-border">
        {ETAPAS.map((etapa) => {
          const linha = porEtapa[etapa.id] ?? { empresas: 0, volume: 0 };
          return (
            <li key={etapa.id} className="px-5 py-3.5">
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
                <p className="font-heading text-[0.86rem] tracking-[-0.01em]">{etapa.label}</p>
                <p className="whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[0.7rem] text-muted-foreground">
                  {linha.empresas} · {compactBRL(linha.volume)}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 h-[2px] w-full bg-border">
                <div
                  className="h-full bg-sea-mid"
                  style={{ width: `${Math.round((linha.volume / maior) * 100)}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/admin/ResultadosBusca.jsx =====
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
import { brl, compactBRL, dataCurta, ETAPAS, MEIO_LABEL, SEGMENTO_LABEL } from "@/lib/app-data";

const ETAPA_LABEL = Object.fromEntries(ETAPAS.map((etapa) => [etapa.id, etapa.label]));

function Grupo({ titulo, contagem, vazio, acao, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border px-5 py-4">
        <h2 className="font-heading text-[0.95rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">{titulo}</h2>
        <span className="font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground">
          {contagem} {contagem === 1 ? "resultado" : "resultados"}
        </span>
      </div>

      {contagem === 0 ? (
        <p className="px-5 py-7 text-[0.8rem] text-muted-foreground">{vazio}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-border">{children}</ul>
      )}

      <div className="border-t border-border px-5 py-3">
        <Link
          to={acao.to}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
        >
          {acao.label}
          <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ResultadosBusca({ empresas, lancamentos, escopo, onLimpar }) {
  const mostrarEmpresas = escopo === "tudo" || escopo === "empresas";
  const mostrarLancamentos = escopo !== "empresas";
  const total =
    (mostrarEmpresas ? empresas.length : 0) + (mostrarLancamentos ? lancamentos.length : 0);

  if (total === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="mt-4 rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card px-5 py-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-[0.86rem] text-foreground">Nenhum resultado encontrado</p>
        <p className="mx-auto mt-1.5 max-w-sm text-[0.76rem] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
          Tente outro nome, CNPJ ou valor. A busca também aceita trechos de valor, como 4.500.
        </p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onLimpar}
          className="mt-4 font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-foreground underline decoration-border underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-foreground"
        >
          Limpar filtros
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div
      className={`mt-4 grid gap-4 ${
        mostrarEmpresas && mostrarLancamentos ? "lg:grid-cols-2 lg:items-start" : ""
      }`}
    >
      {mostrarEmpresas && (
        <Grupo
          titulo={escopo === "empresas" ? "Empresas" : "Empresas encontradas"}
          contagem={empresas.length}
          vazio="Nenhuma empresa corresponde à busca."
          acao={{ to: "/empresas", label: "Abrir funil" }}
        >
          {empresas.map((empresa) => (
            <li key={empresa.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-3.5">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-[0.84rem] text-foreground">{empresa.nome}</p>
                <p className="mt-0.5 truncate font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                  {SEGMENTO_LABEL[empresa.segmento] ?? "—"} ·{" "}
                  {ETAPA_LABEL[empresa.etapa] ?? "Auditoria"}
                  {empresa.cnpj ? ` · ${empresa.cnpj}` : ""}
                </p>
              </div>
              <p className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[0.78rem] text-foreground">
                {compactBRL(empresa.volume_mensal)}
                <span className="text-muted-foreground"> /mês</span>
              </p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </Grupo>
      )}

      {mostrarLancamentos && (
        <Grupo
          titulo={escopo === "alto" ? "Lançamentos acima de R$ 10 mil" : "Lançamentos encontrados"}
          contagem={lancamentos.length}
          vazio={
            escopo === "alto"
              ? "Nenhum lançamento acima de R$ 10 mil."
              : "Nenhum lançamento corresponde à busca."
          }
          acao={{ to: "/extrato", label: "Ver extrato" }}
        >
          {lancamentos.map((lancamento) => (
            <li key={lancamento.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-3.5">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-[0.84rem] text-foreground/90">{lancamento.descricao}</p>
                <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                  {dataCurta(lancamento.data)} · {MEIO_LABEL[lancamento.meio] ?? lancamento.meio} ·{" "}
                  {lancamento.status}
                </p>
              </div>
              <p
                className={`shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[0.8rem] ${
                  lancamento.natureza === "saida" ? "text-muted-foreground" : "text-foreground"
                }`}
              >
                {lancamento.natureza === "saida" ? "−" : "+"}
                {brl(lancamento.valor, 2)}
              </p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </Grupo>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/admin/TrilhaTabela.jsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { brl, dataCurta } from "@/lib/app-data";

const filtros = [
  { id: "todas", label: "Todas" },
  { id: "pago", label: "Pagas" },
  { id: "ativo", label: "Aguardando" },
];

const statusTone = {
  pago: "text-positive",
  ativo: "text-secondary",
  expirado: "text-muted-foreground",
};

const statusLabel = { pago: "liquidado", ativo: "aguardando", expirado: "expirado" };

export default function TrilhaTabela({ itens }) {
  const [filtro, setFiltro] = useState("todas");
  const [termo, setTermo] = useState("");

  const lista = useMemo(() => {
    const busca = termo.trim().toLowerCase();
    return itens.filter((item) => {
      if (filtro !== "todas" && item.status !== filtro) return false;
      if (!busca) return true;
      return [item.cliente, item.codigo, item.titulo, item.recebedor]
        .join(" ")
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(busca);
    });
  }, [itens, filtro, termo]);

  return (
    <div className="mt-4 rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-border px-5 py-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
        <div>
          <h2 className="font-heading text-[0.95rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.02em]">
            Trilha das cobranças
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[0.74rem] text-muted-foreground">
            Quem gerou o link, para qual chave o Pix foi e quanto ficou de tarifa.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
          {filtros.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFiltro(f.id)}
              className={`rounded-[2px] border px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] transition-colors ${
                filtro === f.id
                  ? "border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground"
                  : "border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-foreground/40 hover:text-foreground"
              }`}
            >
              {f.label}
            </button>
          ))}
          <input
            value={termo}
            onChange={(evento) => setTermo(evento.target.value)}
            placeholder="cliente, código ou recebedor"
            className="w-full rounded-[2px] border border-border bg-background px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-foreground placeholder:text-muted-foreground focus:border-foreground/40 focus:outline-none sm:w-56"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full min-w-[1000px] border-collapse text-left">
          <thead>
            <tr className="font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground">
              <th className="px-5 py-3 font-normal">Cliente</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 font-normal">Cobrança</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 font-normal">Destino do Pix</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 text-right font-normal">Valor</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 text-right font-normal">Tarifa Payonda</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 text-right font-normal">Líquido</th>
              <th className="px-5 py-3 text-right font-normal">Situação</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {lista.map((item) => (
              <tr key={item.id} className="border-t border-border">
                <td className="px-5 py-3">
                  <p className="text-[0.8rem] text-foreground/90">{item.cliente}</p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.58rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                    {dataCurta(String(item.criado_em ?? "").slice(0, 10))}
                  </p>
                </td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3">
                  <Link
                    to="/pagar/$codigo"
                    params={{ codigo: item.codigo }}
                    target="_blank"
                    className="font-mono text-[0.68rem] text-foreground underline decoration-border underline-offset-4 hover:decoration-foreground"
                  >
                    {item.codigo}
                  </Link>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 max-w-[220px] truncate text-[0.76rem] text-muted-foreground">
                    {item.titulo}
                  </p>
                </td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3">
                  <p className="text-[0.78rem] text-foreground/85">{item.recebedor || "—"}</p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.6rem] text-muted-foreground">{item.chave}</p>
                </td>
                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-right font-mono text-[0.78rem]">
                  {brl(item.valor, 2)}
                </td>
                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-right font-mono text-[0.78rem] text-secondary">
                  {item.taxa > 0 ? brl(item.taxa, 2) : "—"}
                </td>
                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-right font-mono text-[0.78rem] text-positive">
                  {item.liquido > 0 ? brl(item.liquido, 2) : "—"}
                </td>
                <td className="px-5 py-3 text-right">
                  <p
                    className={`font-mono text-[0.58rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] ${
                      statusTone[item.status] ?? "text-muted-foreground"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {statusLabel[item.status] ?? item.status}
                  </p>
                  {item.pago_em && (
                    <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.56rem] text-muted-foreground">
                      {dataCurta(String(item.pago_em).slice(0, 10))}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
            {lista.length === 0 && (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={7} className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-[0.8rem] text-muted-foreground">
                  Nenhuma cobrança encontrada com esse filtro.
                </td>
              </tr>
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/codigo/ListaArquivos.jsx =====
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";

export default function ListaArquivos({ arquivos, selecionado, onSelecionar }) {
  const [busca, setBusca] = useState("");

  const filtrados = useMemo(() => {
    const termo = busca.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!termo) return arquivos;
    return arquivos.filter((arquivo) => arquivo.caminho.toLowerCase().includes(termo));
  }, [arquivos, busca]);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div className="border-b border-border p-2.5">
        <Input
          value={busca}
          onChange={(evento) => setBusca(evento.target.value)}
          placeholder="filtrar arquivo"
          className="h-8 rounded-[2px] font-mono text-[0.66rem]"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-1">
        {filtrados.map((arquivo) => {
          const ativo = arquivo.caminho === selecionado;
          return (
            <button
              key={arquivo.caminho}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelecionar(arquivo.caminho)}
              className={`block w-full truncate px-3 py-1.5 text-left font-mono text-[0.6rem] transition-colors ${
                ativo
                  ? "bg-accent text-foreground"
                  : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-accent/60 hover:text-foreground"
              }`}
            >
              {arquivo.caminho}
            </button>
          );
        })}

        {filtrados.length === 0 && (
          <p className="px-3 py-4 text-[0.74rem] text-muted-foreground">Nenhum arquivo com esse nome.</p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/codigo/VisualizadorArquivo.jsx =====
import { useState } from "react";
import { Check, Copy } from "lucide-react";

export default function VisualizadorArquivo({ arquivo, carregando }) {
  const [copiado, setCopiado] = useState(false);

  if (!arquivo) {
    return (
      <p className="p-4 text-[0.78rem] text-muted-foreground">Selecione um arquivo na lista.</p>
    );
  }

  const copiar = async () => {
    if (!arquivo.conteudo) return;
    await navigator.clipboard.writeText(arquivo.conteudo);
    setCopiado(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiado(false), 1800);
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border px-4 py-2">
        <p className="truncate font-mono text-[0.62rem] text-muted-foreground">{arquivo.caminho}</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={copiar}
          disabled={carregando || !arquivo.conteudo}
          className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-[2px] border border-border px-2.5 py-1.5 font-mono text-[0.54rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {copiado ? <Check className="h-3 w-3" /> : <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />}
          {copiado ? "copiado" : "copiar"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {carregando ? (
        <p className="p-4 font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground">
          lendo arquivo
        </p>
      ) : (
        <pre className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto whitespace-pre p-4 font-mono text-[0.64rem] leading-relaxed text-foreground">
          {arquivo.conteudo}
        </pre>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/equipe/ClienteTabela.jsx =====
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { SEGMENTO_LABEL, brl } from "@/lib/app-data";

const STATUS = {
  pendente: { label: "Pendente", classe: "text-muted-foreground" },
  em_analise: { label: "Em análise", classe: "text-secondary" },
  aprovado: { label: "Aprovado", classe: "text-positive" },
};

function custos(cliente) {
  const partes = [];
  if (cliente.tarifa_pix != null) partes.push(`Pix ${cliente.tarifa_pix}%`);
  if (cliente.tarifa_boleto != null) partes.push(`Boleto ${brl(cliente.tarifa_boleto, 2)}`);
  if (cliente.tarifa_cartao != null) partes.push(`Cartão ${cliente.tarifa_cartao}%`);
  return partes.length ? partes.join(" · ") : "não definido";
}

export default function ClienteTabela({ clientes }) {
  if (!clientes.length) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card px-5 py-10 text-center">
        <p className="text-[0.85rem] text-foreground/85">Nenhum cliente com esses filtros.</p>
        <p className="mt-1.5 font-mono text-[0.55rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
          ajuste a busca ou o status
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <table className="w-full min-w-[860px] border-collapse text-left">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b border-border">
            {["Cliente", "Segmento", "Custos acordados", "Limite mensal", "Status", ""].map((titulo) => (
              <th
                key={titulo}
                className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground"
              >
                {titulo}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-border">
          {clientes.map((cliente) => {
            const status = STATUS[cliente.status] ?? STATUS.pendente;
            return (
              <tr key={cliente.id} className="transition-colors hover:bg-muted/40">
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <p className="text-[0.84rem] text-foreground/90">
                    {cliente.nome_fantasia || cliente.razao_social}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.55rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                    {cliente.cnpj ?? "sem CNPJ"}
                  </p>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-[0.78rem] text-muted-foreground">
                  {SEGMENTO_LABEL[cliente.segmento] ?? "—"}
                </td>
                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 font-mono text-[0.66rem] text-foreground/80">
                  {custos(cliente)}
                </td>
                <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 font-mono text-[0.72rem] text-foreground/80">
                  {cliente.limite_mensal != null ? brl(cliente.limite_mensal) : "—"}
                </td>
                <td className={`whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 font-mono text-[0.55rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] ${status.classe}`}>
                  {status.label}
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                  <Link
                    to="/equipe/$clienteId"
                    params={{ clienteId: cliente.id }}
                    className="font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
                  >
                    abrir ficha
                  </Link>
                </td>
              </tr>
            );
          })}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/equipe/FichaCadastro.jsx =====
import { SEGMENTO_LABEL } from "@/lib/app-data";

function formatarData(iso) {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("pt-BR", { timeZone: "America/Bahia" });
}

function Linha({ rotulo, valor }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4 border-b border-border/60 py-2.5 last:border-0">
      <span className="font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
        {rotulo}
      </span>
      <span className="max-w-[62%] break-words text-right text-[0.8rem] text-foreground/90">
        {valor || "—"}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function FichaCadastro({ cliente }) {
  const local = [cliente.cidade, cliente.uf].filter(Boolean).join(" / ");

  return (
    <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card px-5 py-5">
      <p className="label-mono">Cadastro</p>
      <div className="mt-2">
        <Linha rotulo="Razão social" valor={cliente.razao_social} />
        <Linha rotulo="Nome fantasia" valor={cliente.nome_fantasia} />
        <Linha rotulo="CNPJ" valor={cliente.cnpj} />
        <Linha rotulo="Segmento" valor={SEGMENTO_LABEL[cliente.segmento] ?? cliente.segmento} />
        <Linha rotulo="Site" valor={cliente.site} />
        <Linha rotulo="Responsável" valor={cliente.responsavel} />
        <Linha rotulo="E-mail" valor={cliente.email_contato} />
        <Linha rotulo="Telefone" valor={cliente.telefone} />
        <Linha rotulo="Praça" valor={local} />
        <Linha rotulo="Cadastrado em" valor={formatarData(cliente.created_date)} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { brl } from "@/lib/app-data";
import ParametroEditor from "./ParametroEditor";

const PARAMETROS = [
  { campo: "tarifa_pix", rotulo: "Tarifa Pix", formato: (v) => `${v}% por recebimento` },
  { campo: "tarifa_boleto", rotulo: "Tarifa boleto", formato: (v) => `${brl(v, 2)} fixo` },
  { campo: "tarifa_cartao", rotulo: "Tarifa cartão", formato: (v) => `${v}% por recebimento` },
  { campo: "prazo_liquidacao", rotulo: "Liquidação", formato: (v) => `D+${v}` },
  { campo: "limite_mensal", rotulo: "Limite mensal", formato: (v) => brl(v) },
  { campo: "gestor", rotulo: "Gestor interno", formato: (v) => v },
];

export default function FichaParametros({ cliente, onSaved }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border px-5 py-4">

          <p className="label-mono">Parâmetros do cliente</p>
          <p className="mt-1.5 text-[0.72rem] text-muted-foreground">

          </p>
        </div>
        <ParametroEditor cliente={cliente} onSaved={onSaved} />
      </div>

                {definido ? formato(valor) : "não definido"}
              </p>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <div className="border-t border-border px-5 py-4">
        <p className="font-mono text-[0.48rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
          Notas internas
        </p>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[0.78rem] leading-relaxed text-foreground/85">
          {cliente.observacoes || "Nenhuma nota registrada."}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/equipe/FichaVinculos.jsx =====
import { brl } from "@/lib/app-data";

const DOC_TIPO = {
  contrato_social: "Contrato social",
  cartao_cnpj: "Cartão CNPJ",
  comprovante_endereco: "Comprovante de endereço",
  documento_socio: "Documento de sócio",
};

const DOC_STATUS = {
  em_analise: { label: "em análise", classe: "text-secondary" },
  aprovado: { label: "aprovado", classe: "text-positive" },
  recusado: { label: "recusado", classe: "text-destructive" },
};

function Secao({ titulo, contagem, vazio, children }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-3">
        <p className="font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-muted-foreground">
          {titulo}
        </p>
        <span className="font-mono text-[0.7rem] text-foreground/70">{contagem}</span>
      </div>
      {contagem === 0 ? (
        <p className="px-5 pb-4 text-[0.75rem] text-muted-foreground">{vazio}</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-border border-t border-border">{children}</ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function FichaVinculos({ documentos, contas, links }) {

        {documentos.map((doc) => {
          const status = DOC_STATUS[doc.status] ?? DOC_STATUS.em_analise;
          return (
            <li key={doc.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-2.5">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-[0.78rem] text-foreground/90">
                  {DOC_TIPO[doc.tipo] ?? doc.tipo}

                  {doc.nome_arquivo ?? "arquivo"}
                </p>
              </div>
              <span className={`shrink-0 font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] ${status.classe}`}>
                {status.label}
              </span>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </Secao>

      <Secao titulo="Chaves Pix" contagem={contas.length} vazio="Nenhuma chave de recebimento.">
        {contas.map((conta) => (
          <li key={conta.id} className="px-5 py-2.5">
            <p className="truncate font-mono text-[0.75rem] text-foreground/90">{conta.chave_pix}</p>
            <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
              {conta.tipo_chave ?? "chave"} · {conta.titular ?? "titular"} · {conta.status ?? "—"}
            </p>
          </li>
        ))}
      </Secao>

      <Secao titulo="Links de pagamento" contagem={links.length} vazio="Nenhum link gerado.">
        {links.map((link) => (
          <li key={link.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-2.5">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="truncate text-[0.78rem] text-foreground/90">{link.titulo}</p>
              <p className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                {link.meio ?? "pix"} · {link.status ?? "ativo"} · {link.codigo}
              </p>
            </div>
            <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[0.72rem] text-foreground/85">
              {brl(link.valor, 2)}
            </span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </Secao>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/equipe/ParametroEditor.jsx =====
import { useState } from "react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";

const CAMPOS = [
  { campo: "tarifa_pix", rotulo: "Tarifa Pix (%)", tipo: "number", passo: "0.01" },
  { campo: "tarifa_boleto", rotulo: "Tarifa boleto (R$)", tipo: "number", passo: "0.01" },
  { campo: "tarifa_cartao", rotulo: "Tarifa cartão (%)", tipo: "number", passo: "0.01" },
  { campo: "prazo_liquidacao", rotulo: "Liquidação (dias)", tipo: "number", passo: "1" },
  { campo: "limite_mensal", rotulo: "Limite mensal (R$)", tipo: "number", passo: "100" },
  { campo: "gestor", rotulo: "Gestor interno", tipo: "text" },
];

const vazio = {};

export default function ParametroEditor({ cliente, onSaved }) {
  const [aberto, setAberto] = useState(false);
  const [valores, setValores] = useState(vazio);
  const [salvando, setSalvando] = useState(false);

  function abrir() {
    const iniciais = {};
    CAMPOS.forEach(({ campo }) => {
      iniciais[campo] = cliente[campo] != null ? String(cliente[campo]) : "";
    });
    iniciais.observacoes = cliente.observacoes ?? "";
    setValores(iniciais);
    setAberto(true);
  }

  async function salvar() {
    setSalvando(true);
    const dados = { observacoes: valores.observacoes?.trim() ?? "" };

    CAMPOS.forEach(({ campo, tipo }) => {
      if (tipo === "number") {
        dados[campo] = valores[campo] === "" ? null : Number(valores[campo]);
      } else {
        dados[campo] = valores[campo]?.trim() ?? "";
      }
    });

    const atualizado = await base44.entities.CadastroCliente.update(cliente.id, dados);
    setSalvando(false);
    setAberto(false);
    onSaved(atualizado);
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={aberto} onOpenChange={(novo) => (novo ? abrir() : setAberto(false))}>
      <DialogTrigger asChild>
        <Button variant="outline" size="sm" className="font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em]">
          Ajustar parâmetros
        </Button>
      </DialogTrigger>
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle className="font-heading tracking-[-0.02em]">Parâmetros comerciais</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            {cliente.nome_fantasia || cliente.razao_social} · valores aplicados nas próximas cobranças.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>

        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {CAMPOS.map(({ campo, rotulo, tipo, passo }) => (
            <div key={campo} className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor={campo} className="font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
                {rotulo}
              </Label>
              <Input
                id={campo}
                type={tipo}
                step={passo}
                value={valores[campo] ?? ""}
                onChange={(evento) => setValores((atual) => ({ ...atual, [campo]: evento.target.value }))}
                className="font-mono text-[0.8rem]"
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="observacoes" className="font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
            Notas internas
          </Label>
          <Textarea
            id="observacoes"
            rows={3}
            value={valores.observacoes ?? ""}
            onChange={(evento) => setValores((atual) => ({ ...atual, observacoes: evento.target.value }))}
            placeholder="Acordos, pendências, histórico da negociação"
            className="text-[0.8rem]"
          />
        </div>

        <DialogFooter>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setAberto(false)} disabled={salvando}>
            Cancelar
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={salvar} disabled={salvando}>
            {salvando ? "Salvando" : "Salvar parâmetros"}
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/links/LinkForm.jsx =====
import { useState } from "react";
import { useServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import { criarLink } from "@/lib/link-fns";

const VAZIO = { titulo: "", valor: "", descricao: "", pagador_nome: "", chave_pix: "", recebedor: "" };

const CAMPO =
  "w-full rounded-[2px] border border-border bg-background px-3 py-2.5 text-[0.85rem] text-foreground outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-muted-foreground/60 focus:border-foreground/40";

export default function LinkForm({ onCriado }) {
  const criar = useServerFn(criarLink);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(VAZIO);
  const [salvando, setSalvando] = useState(false);

  const alterar = (campo) => (evento) =>
    setForm((atual) => ({ ...atual, [campo]: evento.target.value }));

  const enviar = async (evento) => {
    evento.preventDefault();
    const valor = Number(String(form.valor).replace(",", "."));

    if (form.titulo.trim().length < 2) return toast.error("Dê um título à cobrança.");
    if (!Number.isFinite(valor) || valor <= 0) return toast.error("Informe um valor maior que zero.");
    if (form.chave_pix.trim().length < 5) return toast.error("Informe a chave Pix que vai receber.");

    setSalvando(true);
    try {
      await criar({
        data: {
          titulo: form.titulo.trim(),
          valor,
          descricao: form.descricao.trim() || undefined,
          pagador_nome: form.pagador_nome.trim() || undefined,
          chave_pix: form.chave_pix.trim(),
          recebedor: form.recebedor.trim() || undefined,
        },
      });
      toast.success("Link criado com Pix ativo.");
      setForm(VAZIO);
      onCriado();
    } catch (erro) {
      toast.error(
        erro?.message === "pix_chave_invalida"
          ? "Essa chave Pix não é válida."
          : "Não foi possível criar o link.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSalvando(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={enviar} className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card px-5 py-5">
      <p className="label-mono">novo link</p>
      <h2 className="mt-2 font-heading text-[1.05rem] font-medium tracking-[-0.01em]">
        Cobrar com Pix
      </h2>

      <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
        <label className="block">
          <span className="label-mono">título</span>
          <input
            value={form.titulo}
            onChange={alterar("titulo")}
            placeholder="Pedido 1042"
            className={`mt-1.5 ${CAMPO}`}
          />
        </label>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <label className="block">
            <span className="label-mono">valor</span>
            <input
              value={form.valor}
              onChange={alterar("valor")}
              inputMode="decimal"
              placeholder="0,00"
              className={`mt-1.5 font-mono ${CAMPO}`}
            />
          </label>
          <label className="block">
            <span className="label-mono">pagador</span>
            <input
              value={form.pagador_nome}
              onChange={alterar("pagador_nome")}
              placeholder="opcional"
              className={`mt-1.5 ${CAMPO}`}
            />
          </label>
        </div>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="label-mono">chave pix que recebe</span>
          <input
            value={form.chave_pix}
            onChange={alterar("chave_pix")}
            placeholder="CNPJ, e-mail, telefone ou chave aleatória"
            className={`mt-1.5 ${CAMPO}`}
          />
        </label>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="label-mono">nome do recebedor · aparece no pix</span>
          <input
            value={form.recebedor}
            onChange={alterar("recebedor")}
            placeholder="razão social da empresa"
            className={`mt-1.5 ${CAMPO}`}
          />
        </label>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="label-mono">descrição</span>
          <textarea
            value={form.descricao}
            onChange={alterar("descricao")}
            rows={2}
            placeholder="O que está sendo cobrado"
            className={`mt-1.5 resize-none ${CAMPO}`}
          />
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-border pt-3">
        <span className="rounded-[2px] border border-positive/45 bg-positive/10 px-2 py-1 font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-positive">
          pix ativo
        </span>
        <span className="rounded-[2px] border border-border px-2 py-1 font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
          boleto · em breve
        </span>
        <span className="rounded-[2px] border border-border px-2 py-1 font-mono text-[0.5rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
          cartão · em breve
        </span>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={salvando}
        className="mt-4 inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-[2px] border border-primary bg-primary px-4 py-2.5 font-mono text-[0.56rem] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/88 disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {salvando && <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />}
        gerar link de pagamento
      </button>
      <p className="mt-2.5 text-[0.72rem] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
        Tarifa de R$ 0,39 por Pix recebido. O pagador não paga nada a mais.
      </p>
    </form>
  );
}

===== src/components/app/links/LinkLista.jsx =====
import { ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";
import BotaoCopiar from "@/components/pagar/BotaoCopiar";
import { brl, dataCurta } from "@/lib/app-data";

const STATUS = {
  ativo: { label: "aguardando", classe: "border-secondary/45 bg-secondary/10 text-secondary" },
  pago: { label: "pago", classe: "border-positive/45 bg-positive/10 text-positive" },
  expirado: { label: "expirado", classe: "border-border bg-muted text-muted-foreground" },
};

const urlPublica = (codigo) => `${window.location.origin}/pagar/${codigo}`;

export default function LinkLista({ links }) {
  if (!links?.length) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card px-5 py-10 text-center">
        <p className="font-heading text-[1rem] font-medium">Nenhum link criado</p>
        <p className="mt-1.5 text-[0.8rem] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
          Gere o primeiro link ao lado e envie para o pagador. O Pix já sai pronto no QR Code.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="rounded-[3px] border border-border bg-card">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-border px-5 py-4">
        <p className="label-mono">links criados</p>
        <p className="font-mono text-[0.55rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-muted-foreground">
          {links.length} no histórico
        </p>
      </div>

      <div>
        {links.map((link) => {
          const status = STATUS[link.status] ?? STATUS.ativo;
          return (
            <div
              key={link.id}
              className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-border px-5 py-4 last:border-b-0 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between"
            >
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-[0.9rem] font-medium">{link.titulo}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[0.52rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                  {link.codigo} · {link.pagador_nome || "pagador não informado"} ·{" "}
                  {dataCurta(String(link.created_date ?? "").slice(0, 10))}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 sm:justify-end">
                <div className="text-right">
                  <p className="whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[0.95rem] tracking-[-0.02em]">
                    {brl(link.valor, 2)}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-[0.48rem] uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-muted-foreground">
                    líquido {brl(link.valor_liquido ?? 0, 2)}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <span
                  className={`shrink-0 rounded-[2px] border px-2 py-1 font-mono text-[0.48rem] uppercase tracking-[0.12em] ${status.classe}`}
                >
                  {status.label}
                </span>

                <BotaoCopiar valor={urlPublica(link.codigo)} rotulo="copiar link" className="shrink-0 py-1.5" />

                <a
                  href={`/pagar/${link.codigo}`}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noreferrer"
                  className="shrink-0 rounded-[2px] border border-border p-1.5 text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-foreground/30 hover:text-foreground"
                  aria-label={`Abrir link ${link.codigo}`}
                >
                  <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
