===== src/components/ui/use-responsive-image.jsx =====
import * as React from "react"
import { useSize } from "../../hooks/use-size"
import { cn } from "../../lib/utils"
import { DEFAULT_TRANSFORM_WIDTH, getImagePreviewClassName } from "./image-helpers"

export function useResponsiveImage({ parsed, fittingType, focalPoint, quality, className, onLoad, onSourceChange }, parentRef) {
  const wrapperRef = React.useRef(null)
  const imgRef = React.useRef(null)
  const size = useSize(wrapperRef)
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = React.useState(false)

  React.useImperativeHandle(parentRef, () => imgRef.current)
  React.useEffect(() => setLoaded(false), [parsed.baseUrl])
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const wrapper = wrapperRef.current
    const replace = (event) => onSourceChange(
      event.detail.src, getImagePreviewClassName(className, wrapper.className, cn("inline-block relative", className))
    )
    wrapper.addEventListener("base44:image-replace", replace)
    return () => wrapper.removeEventListener("base44:image-replace", replace)
  }, [className, onSourceChange])

  const crop = fittingType !== "fit"
  // Wait for useSize's pre-paint measurement before requesting a transform.
  const options = size && {
    width: size.width || DEFAULT_TRANSFORM_WIDTH,
    height: size.height || undefined,
    crop,
    focalPoint: crop ? focalPoint : undefined,
    quality,
  }

  return {
    wrapperRef, imgRef, loaded, options,
    handleLoad: (event) => {
      setLoaded(true)
      onLoad?.(event)
    },
  }
}


===== src/components/ui/use-toast.jsx =====
// Inspired by react-hot-toast library
import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

const TOAST_LIMIT = 20;
const TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000;

const actionTypes = {
  ADD_TOAST: "ADD_TOAST",
  UPDATE_TOAST: "UPDATE_TOAST",
  DISMISS_TOAST: "DISMISS_TOAST",
  REMOVE_TOAST: "REMOVE_TOAST",
};

let count = 0;

function genId() {
  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_VALUE;
  return count.toString();
}

const toastTimeouts = new Map();

const addToRemoveQueue = (toastId) => {
  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {
    return;
  }

  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);
    dispatch({
      type: actionTypes.REMOVE_TOAST,
      toastId,
    });
  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY);

  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout);
};

const _clearFromRemoveQueue = (toastId) => {
  const timeout = toastTimeouts.get(toastId);
  if (timeout) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);
  }
};

export const reducer = (state, action) => {
  switch (action.type) {
    case actionTypes.ADD_TOAST:
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),
      };

    case actionTypes.UPDATE_TOAST:
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t
        ),
      };

    case actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST: {
      const { toastId } = action;

      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,
      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity
      if (toastId) {
        addToRemoveQueue(toastId);
      } else {
        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {
          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id);
        });
      }

      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined
            ? {
                ...t,
                open: false,
              }
            : t
        ),
      };
    }
    case actionTypes.REMOVE_TOAST:
      if (action.toastId === undefined) {
        return {
          ...state,
          toasts: [],
        };
      }
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== action.toastId),
      };
  }
};

const listeners = [];

let memoryState = { toasts: [] };

function dispatch(action) {
  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action);
  listeners.forEach((listener) => {
    listener(memoryState);
  });
}

function toast({ ...props }) {
  const id = genId();

  const update = (props) =>
    dispatch({
      type: actionTypes.UPDATE_TOAST,
      toast: { ...props, id },
    });

  const dismiss = () =>
    dispatch({ type: actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST, toastId: id });

  dispatch({
    type: actionTypes.ADD_TOAST,
    toast: {
      ...props,
      id,
      open: true,
      onOpenChange: (open) => {
        if (!open) dismiss();
      },
    },
  });

  return {
    id,
    dismiss,
    update,
  };
}

function useToast() {
  const [state, setState] = useState(memoryState);

  useEffect(() => {
    listeners.push(setState);
    return () => {
      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState);
      if (index > -1) {
        listeners.splice(index, 1);
      }
    };
  }, [state]);

  return {
    ...state,
    toast,
    dismiss: (toastId) => dispatch({ type: actionTypes.DISMISS_TOAST, toastId }),
  };
}

export { useToast, toast }; 

===== src/hooks/use-carteira.js =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { ETAPAS, ECONOMIA_RATE, TAXA_PAYONDA } from "@/lib/app-data";

/**
 * Visão consolidada da operação: empresas da carteira (por etapa) e lançamentos
 * processados. Usada apenas no painel administrativo.
 */
export function useCarteira() {
  const [empresas, setEmpresas] = useState(null);
  const [lancamentos, setLancamentos] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let ativo = true;
    base44.entities.Empresa.list("-updated_date", 200).then((lista) => {
      if (ativo) setEmpresas(lista ?? []);
    });
    base44.entities.Lancamento.list("-data", 200).then((lista) => {
      if (ativo) setLancamentos(lista ?? []);
    });
    return () => {
      ativo = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const adicionar = (empresa) => setEmpresas((atual) => [empresa, ...(atual ?? [])]);

  const resumo = useMemo(() => {
    const lista = empresas ?? [];
    const aprovados = (lancamentos ?? []).filter(
      (l) => l.natureza !== "saida" && l.status === "aprovado",
    );

    const porEtapa = {};
    ETAPAS.forEach((etapa) => {
      porEtapa[etapa.id] = { empresas: 0, volume: 0 };
    });

    lista.forEach((empresa) => {
      const linha = porEtapa[empresa.etapa ?? "auditoria"] ?? porEtapa.auditoria;
      linha.empresas += 1;
      linha.volume += empresa.volume_mensal ?? 0;
    });

    const volumeCarteira = lista.reduce((soma, e) => soma + (e.volume_mensal ?? 0), 0);
    const volumeAtivo = porEtapa.ativo.volume;
    const emMigracao = ["auditoria", "proposta", "integracao"].reduce(
      (soma, id) => soma + porEtapa[id].empresas,
      0,
    );

    return {
      total: lista.length,
      ativas: porEtapa.ativo.empresas,
      emMigracao,
      volumeCarteira,
      volumeAtivo,
      receitaMensal: volumeAtivo * TAXA_PAYONDA,
      economia: volumeCarteira * ECONOMIA_RATE,
      receitaRealizada: aprovados.reduce((soma, l) => soma + (l.taxa ?? 0), 0),
      volumeProcessado: aprovados.reduce((soma, l) => soma + (l.valor ?? 0), 0),
      aprovados: aprovados.length,
      porEtapa,
    };
  }, [empresas, lancamentos]);

  return { empresas, lancamentos, resumo, adicionar };
}

===== src/hooks/use-conta.js =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";

export function useConta(dias) {
  const [lancamentos, setLancamentos] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let ativo = true;
    base44.entities.Lancamento.list("-data", 200).then((lista) => {
      if (ativo) setLancamentos(lista ?? []);
    });
    return () => {
      ativo = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const resumo = useMemo(() => {
    const lista = lancamentos ?? [];
    const datas = [...new Set(lista.map((l) => l.data).filter(Boolean))].sort();
    const ultima = datas[datas.length - 1];

    let base = lista;
    if (dias && ultima) {
      const limite = new Date(`${ultima}T00:00:00Z`);
      limite.setUTCDate(limite.getUTCDate() - (dias - 1));
      const iso = limite.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
      base = lista.filter((l) => !l.data || l.data >= iso);
    }

    const aprovados = base.filter((l) => l.natureza !== "saida" && l.status === "aprovado");
    const volume = aprovados.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.valor ?? 0), 0);
    const taxa = aprovados.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.taxa ?? 0), 0);
    const taxaMercado = aprovados.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.taxa_mercado ?? 0), 0);
    const recusados = base.filter((l) => l.status === "recusado");
    const saidas = base.filter((l) => l.natureza === "saida");

    const porMeio = ["pix", "cartao", "boleto", "cambio"]
      .map((meio) => {
        const doMeio = aprovados.filter((l) => l.meio === meio);
        return {
          meio,
          valor: doMeio.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.valor ?? 0), 0),
          taxa: doMeio.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.taxa ?? 0), 0),
        };
      })
      .filter((r) => r.valor > 0);

    const diasComMovimento = [...new Set(aprovados.map((l) => l.data))].sort();
    const porDia = diasComMovimento.map((data) => ({
      data,
      valor: aprovados.filter((l) => l.data === data).reduce((s, l) => s + (l.valor ?? 0), 0),
    }));

    return {
      volume,
      taxa,
      economia: Math.max(taxaMercado - taxa, 0),
      custoPct: volume ? (taxa / volume) * 100 : 0,
      bloqueios: recusados.length,
      protegido: recusados.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.valor ?? 0), 0),
      saidas: saidas.reduce((s, l) => s + (l.valor ?? 0), 0),
      porMeio,
      porDia,
    };
  }, [lancamentos, dias]);

  return { lancamentos, resumo };
}

===== src/hooks/use-mobile.jsx =====
import * as React from "react"

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState(undefined)

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    }
    mql.addEventListener("change", onChange)
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
    return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
  }, [])

  return !!isMobile
}


===== src/hooks/use-size.jsx =====
import * as React from "react"

export function useSize(ref) {
  const [size, setSize] = React.useState(null)

  // useLayoutEffect (not useEffect): the initial measurement must land before
  // the browser paints, so consumers can render their real content on the
  // very first painted frame instead of a guess. A ResizeObserver's first
  // callback arrives too late for that — by then an <img> src guess has
  // already been dispatched to the network.
  React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const element = ref.current
    if (!element) return

    const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect()
    setSize({ width: rect.width, height: rect.height })

    const observer = new ResizeObserver(([entry]) => {
      const { width, height } = entry.contentRect
      setSize({ width, height })
    })

    observer.observe(element)
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [ref])

  return size
}

  /* Institutional label: the small mono eyebrow used across the site. */
  .label-mono {
    @apply font-mono text-[0.6rem] uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-muted-foreground;
  }

  /* Grafite com profundidade de mar: o preto da marca ganha o azul nas bordas. */
  .sea-surface {
    background-image: radial-gradient(115% 85% at 82% 4%, hsl(var(--sea-deep) / 0.55), transparent 58%),
      radial-gradient(75% 62% at 6% 0%, hsl(var(--sea-mid) / 0.16), transparent 62%);
  }

  .sea-drift {
    animation: sea-drift 34s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
  }
}

@keyframes sea-drift {
  from {
    transform: translate3d(-2.5%, 0, 0) scale(1.06);
  }
  to {
    transform: translate3d(2.5%, -1.5%, 0) scale(1.06);
  }
}

===== src/lib/AuthContext.jsx =====
import { queryOptions, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createContext, useContext, useMemo } from "react";
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { currentUrl } from "@/lib/authReturnTo";

/**
 * The visitor, resolved once per page load and cached in the router's QueryClient:
 * the login gate (src/components/auth/AuthGate.jsx) reads it with
 * `context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(authQuery)`, components with `useAuth()`.
 * Resolves to `{ user, error }`: an anonymous visitor (no token, or a 401/403) is `{ user: null, error: null }`.
 * Anything else, an outage (429, 503) or a request that never got an answer, rejects instead,
 * so it is never cached as "signed out" and the next read asks again.
 */
export const authQuery = queryOptions({
  queryKey: ["auth", "me"],
  queryFn: async () => {
    // No token means no session; /me could only answer 401.
    if (!base44.auth.hasToken()) return { user: null, error: null };
    try {
      return { user: await base44.auth.me(), error: null };
    } catch (err) {
      if (err?.data?.extra_data?.reason === "user_not_registered") {
        return { user: null, error: { type: "user_not_registered", message: "Not registered" } };
      }
      if (err?.status === 401 || err?.status === 403) return { user: null, error: null };
      throw err;
    }
  },
  staleTime: Infinity,
  retry: false,
});

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

/**
 * Client-side session state. The server renders every page anonymously and the
 * query only runs in the browser after hydration, so the first client render
 * matches the server markup. Gate user-specific UI on `isLoadingAuth`.
 */
export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data, error, isPending } = useQuery(authQuery);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      user: data?.user ?? null,
      isAuthenticated: !!data?.user,
      isLoadingAuth: isPending,
      authError: data?.error ?? (error ? { type: "unknown", message: error.message ?? "Failed to load session" } : null),
      refresh: () => queryClient.fetchQuery({ ...authQuery, staleTime: 0 }),
      logout: () => {
        queryClient.setQueryData(authQuery.queryKey, { user: null, error: null });
        base44.auth.logout(window.location.origin);
      },
      // Full-page sign-in: /login with the current page as the way back. A hard
      // navigation on purpose — on an app served behind the platform login the
      // platform answers /login itself; an app with its own auth pages serves its /login route.
      navigateToLogin: () => base44.auth.redirectToLogin(currentUrl()),
    }),
    [data, error, isPending, queryClient],
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth() {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAuth must be used within <AuthProvider>");
  return ctx;
}


===== src/lib/ai.server.js =====
import { openaiCompatibleText } from "@tanstack/ai-openai/compatible";

// A TanStack AI chat adapter on the Base44 AI gateway: OpenAI-compatible, no API
// key, billed to the app's AI credits. Pass `context.getBase44()`. The gateway is
// called as the app (service role) because an owner can restrict it to server-side
// calls, which refuses a visitor's token, so authenticate the caller first.
// "automatic" lets the platform pick the model; named models cost more credits.
export const gatewayModel = (base44, model = "automatic") => {
  const { baseURL, token, headers } = base44.asServiceRole.aiGateway.connection();
  return openaiCompatibleText(model, { name: "base44", baseURL, apiKey: token, defaultHeaders: headers });
};

  servicos: "Serviços",

// 2,27% de mercado contra 1,26% na Payonda = 1,01 p.p. de economia
export const ECONOMIA_RATE = 0.0101;

// Custo total cobrado pela Payonda: 1,26% do volume processado
export const TAXA_PAYONDA = 0.0126;

export function brl(value, digits = 0) {
  return (value ?? 0).toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "BRL",
    minimumFractionDigits: digits,
    maximumFractionDigits: digits,
  });
}

export function pct(value, digits = 2) {
  return `${(value ?? 0).toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
    minimumFractionDigits: digits,
    maximumFractionDigits: digits,
  })}%`;
}

export function compactBRL(value) {
  const v = value ?? 0;
  if (Math.abs(v) >= 1000000) {
    return `R$ ${(v / 1000000).toLocaleString("pt-BR", { maximumFractionDigits: 1 })} mi`;
  }
  if (Math.abs(v) >= 1000) {
    return `R$ ${(v / 1000).toLocaleString("pt-BR", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })} mil`;
  }
  return brl(v);
}

export function dataCurta(iso) {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  const [ano, mes, dia] = iso.split("-");
  return `${dia}/${mes}`;
}

===== src/lib/app-params.js =====
import { getAccessToken } from "@base44/sdk";

const isBrowser = typeof window !== "undefined";

const isClearAccessTokenRequested = () =>
  isBrowser && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("clear_access_token") === "true";

const clearStoredAccessToken = () => {
  window.localStorage.removeItem("base44_access_token");
  window.localStorage.removeItem("token");
};

export function getAppParams() {
  if (isClearAccessTokenRequested()) clearStoredAccessToken();
  return {
    appId: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_APP_ID,
    token: isBrowser ? getAccessToken() : null,
    functionsVersion: (isBrowser && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("functions_version")?.trim()) || import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_FUNCTIONS_VERSION,
    appBaseUrl: import.meta.env.VITE_BASE44_APP_BASE_URL,
  };
}


===== src/lib/ativos-app-store.js =====
/**
 * Ativos da App Store desenhados em canvas, nos tamanhos que o App Store
 * Connect aceita. O desenho usa unidades relativas (100 = largura do arquivo),
 * então o mesmo layout serve para qualquer resolução declarada em ATIVOS.
 */

const COR = {
  fundo: "#09090a",
  card: "#0f1012",
  borda: "#26262b",
  texto: "#edece8",
  muted: "#8f8f96",
  seaDeep: "#305469",
  seaMid: "#3c92aa",
  seaLight: "#85ced6",
  positivo: "#579f7d",
  ouro: "#c9b483",
};

const MONO = '"JetBrains Mono", monospace';
const SANS = '"Inter Tight", sans-serif';
const SERIF = '"Instrument Serif", Georgia, serif';

function rotulo(ctx, texto, x, y, tamanho = 1.05, cor = COR.muted) {
  ctx.fillStyle = cor;
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
  ctx.font = `500 ${tamanho}px ${MONO}`;
  if ("letterSpacing" in ctx) ctx.letterSpacing = `${(tamanho * 0.18).toFixed(2)}px`;
  ctx.fillText(texto.toUpperCase(), x, y);
  if ("letterSpacing" in ctx) ctx.letterSpacing = "0px";
}

function texto(ctx, conteudo, x, y, { tamanho = 2, peso = 400, cor = COR.texto, familia = SANS } = {}) {
  ctx.fillStyle = cor;
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
  ctx.font = `${peso} ${tamanho}px ${familia}`;
  ctx.fillText(conteudo, x, y);
}

function paragrafo(ctx, conteudo, x, y, { largura, tamanho = 1.6, entrelinha = 2.3, cor = COR.muted, peso = 400, familia = SANS }) {
  ctx.fillStyle = cor;
  ctx.textAlign = "left";
  ctx.textBaseline = "alphabetic";
  ctx.font = `${peso} ${tamanho}px ${familia}`;

  const palavras = String(conteudo).split(" ");
  let linha = "";
  let altura = y;
  for (const palavra of palavras) {
    const teste = linha ? `${linha} ${palavra}` : palavra;
    if (ctx.measureText(teste).width > largura && linha) {
      ctx.fillText(linha, x, altura);
      linha = palavra;
      altura += entrelinha;
    } else {
      linha = teste;
    }
  }
  if (linha) ctx.fillText(linha, x, altura);
  return altura;
}

function fundo(ctx, largura, altura) {
  ctx.fillStyle = COR.fundo;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, largura, altura);

  const brilho = ctx.createRadialGradient(
    largura * 0.88,
    0,
    0,
    largura * 0.88,
    0,
    Math.max(largura, altura) * 0.85,
  );
  brilho.addColorStop(0, "rgba(48, 84, 105, 0.55)");
  brilho.addColorStop(0.55, "rgba(48, 84, 105, 0.14)");
  brilho.addColorStop(1, "rgba(9, 9, 10, 0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = brilho;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, largura, altura);
}

function ondas(ctx, largura, altura, { base = 0.72, escala = 1 } = {}) {
  const faixas = [
    { y: altura * base, amp: altura * 0.035 * escala, cor: "rgba(60, 146, 170, 0.55)", lw: 0.22 },
    { y: altura * (base + 0.055), amp: altura * 0.03 * escala, cor: "rgba(133, 206, 214, 0.28)", lw: 0.18 },
    { y: altura * (base + 0.11), amp: altura * 0.026 * escala, cor: "rgba(48, 84, 105, 0.7)", lw: 0.18 },
  ];

  for (const faixa of faixas) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(-6, faixa.y);
    const passo = (largura + 12) / 4;
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
      const x = -6 + passo * i;
      ctx.bezierCurveTo(
        x + passo * 0.3,
        faixa.y - faixa.amp,
        x + passo * 0.7,
        faixa.y + faixa.amp,
        x + passo,
        faixa.y,
      );
    }
    ctx.strokeStyle = faixa.cor;
    ctx.lineWidth = faixa.lw;
    ctx.stroke();
  }
}

function linha(ctx, x, y, ate, cor = COR.borda) {
  ctx.strokeStyle = cor;
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.15;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x, y);
  ctx.lineTo(ate, y);
  ctx.stroke();
}

function cartao(ctx, x, y, largura, altura, raio = 1.6) {
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(x + raio, y);
  ctx.arcTo(x + largura, y, x + largura, y + altura, raio);
  ctx.arcTo(x + largura, y + altura, x, y + altura, raio);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y + altura, x, y, raio);
  ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + largura, y, raio);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = COR.card;
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = COR.borda;
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.16;
  ctx.stroke();
}

function selo(ctx, conteudo, x, y, { cor = COR.positivo, fundoCor = "rgba(87, 159, 125, 0.14)", tamanho = 0.95 } = {}) {
  ctx.font = `500 ${tamanho}px ${MONO}`;
  const largura = ctx.measureText(conteudo.toUpperCase()).width + tamanho * 1.8;
  const altura = tamanho * 2.5;

  ctx.fillStyle = fundoCor;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(x, y, largura, altura, 0.6);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = cor;
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.12;
  ctx.stroke();

  rotulo(ctx, conteudo, x + tamanho * 0.9, y + altura * 0.68, tamanho, cor);
  return largura;
}

function qrEstilizado(ctx, x, y, tamanho, semente = 7) {
  ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.roundRect(x, y, tamanho, tamanho, 0.8);
  ctx.fill();

  const modulos = 25;
  const unidade = (tamanho * 0.9) / modulos;
  const inicio = tamanho * 0.05;
  let estado = semente;

  for (let linhaAtual = 0; linhaAtual < modulos; linhaAtual += 1) {
    for (let coluna = 0; coluna < modulos; coluna += 1) {
      estado = (estado * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2147483648;
      const pintar =
        (linhaAtual < 5 && coluna < 5) ||
        (linhaAtual < 5 && coluna > modulos - 6) ||
        (linhaAtual > modulos - 6 && coluna < 5) ||
        (linhaAtual > 4 && coluna > 4 && estado % 100 > 46);

      if (!pintar) continue;
      ctx.fillStyle = "#101012";
      ctx.fillRect(
        x + inicio + coluna * unidade,
        y + inicio + linhaAtual * unidade,
        unidade * 0.96,
        unidade * 0.96,
      );
    }
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ ícone */

/** Ícone: 1024 × 1024 px exatos, fundo opaco, marca em vetor, sem alpha. */
function desenharIcone(ctx, largura, altura) {
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, largura, altura);
  ctx.fillStyle = COR.fundo;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, largura, altura);

  const brilho = ctx.createRadialGradient(largura * 0.85, 0, 0, largura * 0.85, 0, largura);
  brilho.addColorStop(0, "rgba(48, 84, 105, 0.62)");
  brilho.addColorStop(0.6, "rgba(48, 84, 105, 0.12)");
  brilho.addColorStop(1, "rgba(9, 9, 10, 0)");
  ctx.fillStyle = brilho;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, largura, altura);

  const escala = (largura * 0.56) / 34;
  const mar = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0.35 * 34 * escala, 34 * escala);
  mar.addColorStop(0, COR.seaLight);
  mar.addColorStop(0.55, COR.seaMid);
  mar.addColorStop(1, COR.seaDeep);

  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(largura / 2 - 17 * escala, altura / 2 - 17 * escala);
  ctx.scale(escala, escala);

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(17, 17, 16.2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(48, 84, 105, 0.55)";
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.9;
  ctx.stroke();

  const faixas = [
    { y: 12.4, opacidade: 1 },
    { y: 17.6, opacidade: 0.6 },
    { y: 22.8, opacidade: 0.3 },
  ];

  for (const faixa of faixas) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(4, faixa.y);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(7.4, faixa.y, 8.7, faixa.y - 4.2, 12.1, faixa.y - 4.2);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(15.5, faixa.y - 4.2, 16.8, faixa.y, 20.2, faixa.y);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(23.6, faixa.y, 24.2, faixa.y - 3.4, 26.5, faixa.y - 3.4);
    ctx.globalAlpha = faixa.opacidade;
    ctx.strokeStyle = mar;
    ctx.lineWidth = 1.9;
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
  ctx.restore();
}

/* ------------------------------------------- resultados da busca (paisagem) */

function busca(ctx, largura, altura) {
  fundo(ctx, largura, altura);
  ondas(ctx, largura, altura, { base: 0.88, escala: 0.55 });

  texto(ctx, "PAYONDA", 5.7, 13.5, { tamanho: 2.5, peso: 600, familia: SANS });
  rotulo(ctx, "conta pj · menor custo total", 5.7, 17.4, 0.8);

  texto(ctx, "Pague menos.", 5.7, 33, { tamanho: 7.8, familia: SERIF });
  texto(ctx, "Receba tudo.", 5.7, 46, { tamanho: 7.8, familia: SERIF });

  paragrafo(
    ctx,
    "Pix, boleto, cartão e câmbio na mesma conta, com a tarifa declarada antes de cada transação.",
    5.7,
    53.5,
    { largura: 52, tamanho: 1.6, entrelinha: 2.2 },
  );

  ctx.strokeStyle = COR.borda;
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.16;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(64.5, 16);
  ctx.lineTo(64.5, 53);
  ctx.stroke();

  rotulo(ctx, "tarifa pix recebido", 68, 21, 0.8);
  texto(ctx, "R$ 0,39", 68, 28.5, { tamanho: 3.7, peso: 500, familia: MONO });
  rotulo(ctx, "mesma cobrança no mercado", 68, 32.5, 0.72);
  texto(ctx, "R$ 0,99", 68, 38.5, { tamanho: 2.5, peso: 500, cor: COR.muted, familia: MONO });

  linha(ctx, 68, 42, 94.5);

  rotulo(ctx, "a cada 1.000 cobranças", 68, 46.5, 0.72);
  texto(ctx, "R$ 600 economizados", 68, 51.5, { tamanho: 1.7, peso: 500, cor: COR.positivo });
}

/* ------------------------------------------------- capturas de tela (retrato) */

function topoCaptura(ctx, altura, titulo, subtitulo) {
  rotulo(ctx, "payonda · conta pj", 6, 8.4, 1.05);
  let y = paragrafo(ctx, titulo, 6, 18.5, {
    largura: 88,
    tamanho: 4.1,
    entrelinha: 4.9,
    cor: COR.texto,
    peso: 500,
  });
  y = paragrafo(ctx, subtitulo, 6, y + 4.4, {
    largura: 82,
    tamanho: 1.7,
    entrelinha: 2.4,
    cor: COR.muted,
  });
  return Math.min(y, altura * 0.24);
}

function rodapeCaptura(ctx, largura, altura) {
  linha(ctx, 6, altura - 6.4, largura - 6);
  rotulo(ctx, "thepayonda.com", 6, altura - 3.2, 1.0);
  rotulo(ctx, "pix · boleto · cartão · câmbio", largura - 6 - 27, altura - 3.2, 1.0);
}

function capturaCusto(ctx, largura, altura) {
  fundo(ctx, largura, altura);
  ondas(ctx, largura, altura, { base: 0.9, escala: 0.7 });

  const topo = topoCaptura(
    ctx,
    altura,
    "Custo total, não tarifa de vitrine.",
    "Cada real processado mostra quanto custou e quanto o mercado cobraria pelo mesmo volume.",
  );

  const x = 6;
  const y = topo + 4.5;
  const w = 88;
  const h = altura - 8 - y;
  cartao(ctx, x, y, w, h);

  const px = x + 5;
  rotulo(ctx, "volume processado · 30 dias", px, y + h * 0.09, 1.05);
  texto(ctx, "R$ 42.318", px, y + h * 0.215, { tamanho: 7.6, peso: 500, familia: MONO });

  linha(ctx, px, y + h * 0.265, x + w - 5);

  rotulo(ctx, "custo total da conta", px, y + h * 0.335, 1.05);
  texto(ctx, "1,26%", px, y + h * 0.425, { tamanho: 5.4, peso: 500 });
  rotulo(ctx, "mercado", px + 18, y + h * 0.335, 1.05);
  texto(ctx, "2,27%", px + 18, y + h * 0.425, { tamanho: 5.4, peso: 500, cor: COR.muted });

  selo(ctx, "+ R$ 2.940 economizados", px, y + h * 0.475, { tamanho: 1.1 });

  const graficoX = px;
  const graficoL = w - 10;
  const graficoA = h * 0.3;
  const graficoY = y + h * 0.6;
  const pontos = [0.32, 0.46, 0.4, 0.58, 0.52, 0.7, 0.64, 0.82, 0.76, 0.95];

  const area = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, graficoY, 0, graficoY + graficoA);
  area.addColorStop(0, "rgba(60, 146, 170, 0.34)");
  area.addColorStop(1, "rgba(60, 146, 170, 0.02)");

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(graficoX, graficoY + graficoA);
  pontos.forEach((valor, indice) => {
    const px2 = graficoX + (graficoL / (pontos.length - 1)) * indice;
    ctx.lineTo(px2, graficoY + graficoA - valor * graficoA);
  });
  ctx.lineTo(graficoX + graficoL, graficoY + graficoA);
  ctx.closePath();
  ctx.fillStyle = area;
  ctx.fill();

  ctx.beginPath();
  pontos.forEach((valor, indice) => {
    const px2 = graficoX + (graficoL / (pontos.length - 1)) * indice;
    const py = graficoY + graficoA - valor * graficoA;
    if (indice === 0) ctx.moveTo(px2, py);
    else ctx.lineTo(px2, py);
  });
  ctx.strokeStyle = COR.seaMid;
  ctx.lineWidth = 0.35;
  ctx.stroke();

  rotulo(ctx, "hoje", graficoX, graficoY + graficoA + 3.6, 1.0);
  rodapeCaptura(ctx, largura, altura);
}

function capturaPix(ctx, largura, altura) {
  fundo(ctx, largura, altura);
  ondas(ctx, largura, altura, { base: 0.9, escala: 0.7 });

  const topo = topoCaptura(
    ctx,
    altura,
    "Cobre com Pix em segundos.",
    "Gere o link, envie no WhatsApp e receba com R$ 0,39 de tarifa. O pagador não paga nada a mais.",
  );

  const x = 6;
  const y = topo + 4.5;
  const w = 88;
  const h = altura - 8 - y;
  cartao(ctx, x, y, w, h);

  const px = x + 5;
  rotulo(ctx, "link de pagamento", px, y + h * 0.075, 1.05);
  texto(ctx, "Pedido 1042", px, y + h * 0.15, { tamanho: 3.6, peso: 500 });
  texto(ctx, "R$ 249,90", px, y + h * 0.245, { tamanho: 7.2, peso: 500, familia: MONO });
  selo(ctx, "pix ativo", px, y + h * 0.275, { tamanho: 1.05 });

  const qr = Math.min(21, w * 0.26);
  qrEstilizado(ctx, px, y + h * 0.43, qr, 11);

  const coluna = px + qr + 5;
  rotulo(ctx, "tarifa payonda", coluna, y + h * 0.47, 1.0);
  texto(ctx, "R$ 0,39", coluna, y + h * 0.55, { tamanho: 4.4, peso: 500 });
  rotulo(ctx, "mesma cobrança no mercado", coluna, y + h * 0.615, 0.95);
  texto(ctx, "R$ 0,99", coluna, y + h * 0.695, { tamanho: 4.4, peso: 500, cor: COR.muted });

  linha(ctx, px, y + h * 0.775, x + w - 5);

  rotulo(ctx, "você recebe", px, y + h * 0.845, 1.05);
  texto(ctx, "R$ 249,51", px + 12.5, y + h * 0.85, { tamanho: 3.6, peso: 500, cor: COR.positivo });

  rodapeCaptura(ctx, largura, altura);
}

function capturaPonda(ctx, largura, altura) {
  fundo(ctx, largura, altura);
  ondas(ctx, largura, altura, { base: 0.9, escala: 0.7 });

  const topo = topoCaptura(
    ctx,
    altura,
    "A Ponda mostra onde você paga caro.",
    "Sua assessora financeira lê os números da conta e aponta o próximo passo, com a tarifa na frente.",
  );

  const x = 6;
  const y = topo + 4.5;
  const w = 88;
  const h = altura - 8 - y;
  cartao(ctx, x, y, w, h);

  const px = x + 5;
  rotulo(ctx, "ponda · briefing de hoje", px, y + h * 0.075, 1.05);
  paragrafo(ctx, "Três pontos onde o custo está acima do necessário.", px, y + h * 0.145, {
    largura: w - 10,
    tamanho: 2.6,
    entrelinha: 3.4,
    cor: COR.texto,
    peso: 500,
  });

  const linhas = [
    ["01", "Boleto", "R$ 0,99 por boleto. O mercado cobra R$ 2,49 no mesmo volume."],
    ["02", "Cartão", "2,79% contra 3,99% de referência. Migrar a captura economiza R$ 1.180."],
    ["03", "Caixa parado", "R$ 18.400 sem render. Aplicar no CDI cobre a tarifa do mês."],
  ];

  linhas.forEach(([numero, titulo, corpo], indice) => {
    const base = y + h * (0.32 + indice * 0.19);
    if (indice > 0) linha(ctx, px, base - 6.4, x + w - 5);
    rotulo(ctx, numero, px, base, 1.05, COR.ouro);
    texto(ctx, titulo, px + 7, base + 0.2, { tamanho: 2.5, peso: 500 });
    paragrafo(ctx, corpo, px + 7, base + 3.6, {
      largura: w - 18,
      tamanho: 1.65,
      entrelinha: 2.3,
    });
  });

  rotulo(ctx, "conteúdo informativo · não é recomendação de investimento", px, y + h * 0.94, 0.85);

  rodapeCaptura(ctx, largura, altura);
}

function capturas(id, nome, tamanho) {
  return [
    { id: `${id}-1-custo-total`, nome: `${nome} 1 · custo total`, tamanho, desenhar: capturaCusto },
    { id: `${id}-2-link-pix`, nome: `${nome} 2 · link de pagamento`, tamanho, desenhar: capturaPix },
    { id: `${id}-3-ponda`, nome: `${nome} 3 · assessora Ponda`, tamanho, desenhar: capturaPonda },
  ];
}

function ativo({ id, nome, largura, altura, desenhar, pixel }) {
  return {
    id,
    nome,
    tamanho: `${largura} × ${altura} px`,
    largura,
    altura,
    desenhar,
    pixel: Boolean(pixel),
  };
}

export const ATIVOS = [
  ativo({
    id: "icone-app-store",
    nome: "Ícone do app",
    largura: 1024,
    altura: 1024,
    desenhar: desenharIcone,
    pixel: true,
  }),
  ativo({
    id: "resultados-da-busca",
    nome: "Resultados da busca",
    largura: 1920,
    altura: 1280,
    desenhar: busca,
  }),
  ...capturas("captura-2007", "Captura 2007×2853", "2007 × 2853 px").map((item) =>
    ativo({ ...item, largura: 2007, altura: 2853 }),
  ),
  ...capturas("captura-1398", "Captura 1398×2034", "1398 × 2034 px").map((item) =>
    ativo({ ...item, largura: 1398, altura: 2034 }),
  ),
  ...capturas("captura-ipad-13", "Captura iPad 13\"", "2064 × 2752 px").map((item) =>
    ativo({ ...item, largura: 2064, altura: 2752 }),
  ),
];

export function desenharAtivo(ativoAtual, ctx) {
  const { largura, altura } = ativoAtual;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, largura, altura);

  if (ativoAtual.pixel) {
    ativoAtual.desenhar(ctx, largura, altura);
  } else {
    const escala = largura / 100;
    ctx.scale(escala, escala);
    ativoAtual.desenhar(ctx, 100, altura / escala);
  }

  ctx.restore();
}

===== src/lib/auth-middleware.js =====
import { createClientFromRequest, getAccessToken } from "@base44/sdk";
import { createMiddleware } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { getRequest, setResponseStatus } from "@tanstack/react-start/server";

// Both middlewares hand handlers the same lazy `context.getBase44()`: a
// function, not the client, because the client needs the platform headers and
// a handler that never touches Base44 must still answer without them. The
// platform sets those headers on every request that reaches the server; a
// visitor's Authorization header makes the client act as that visitor.
const withGetBase44 = (next, request = getRequest()) => {
  let client;
  return next({ context: { getBase44: () => (client ??= createClientFromRequest(request)) } });
};

// Applied to every server function by src/start.js: sends the visitor's Base44
// access token with the call so `context.getBase44()` acts as the visitor (entity
// access rules apply). Anonymous visitors send nothing and get an anonymous client.
export const authMiddleware = createMiddleware({ type: "function" })
  .client(async ({ next }) => {
    const token = typeof window === "undefined" ? null : getAccessToken();
    return next({ headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {} });
  })
  .server(({ next }) => withGetBase44(next));

// Applied to every request by src/start.js, so a server route handler
// (`server.handlers`) receives the same `context.getBase44()` as a server function.
export const base44RequestMiddleware = createMiddleware({ type: "request" }).server(({ next, request }) =>
  withGetBase44(next, request),
);

// Only an auth rejection means "not signed in"; outages and rate limits propagate as themselves.
const currentUser = (context) =>
  context
    .getBase44()
    .auth.me()
    .catch((error) => {
      if (error?.status === 401 || error?.status === 403) return null;
      throw error;
    });

// Neither middleware authenticates anyone. Add this to a server function that
// needs a signed-in caller; it rejects anonymous callers and sets `context.user`:
//   createServerFn().middleware([requireUser]).handler(({ context }) => context.user.id)
export const requireUser = createMiddleware({ type: "function" }).server(async ({ next, context }) => {
  const user = await currentUser(context);
  if (!user) {
    setResponseStatus(401);
    throw Object.assign(new Error("Unauthorized"), { status: 401, code: "UNAUTHORIZED" });
  }
  return next({ context: { user } });
});

// The same for a server route: `server: { middleware: [requireUserRoute], handlers }` answers
// anonymous callers with 401 and sets `context.user`. Only on src/routes/api/* files: a route's
// middleware also runs for every route under it, and page navigations carry no token.
export const requireUserRoute = createMiddleware({ type: "request" }).server(async ({ next, context }) => {
  const user = await currentUser(context);
  if (!user) return Response.json({ error: "Unauthorized" }, { status: 401 });
  return next({ context: { user } });
});


===== src/lib/authMfa.js =====
import { base44 } from "@/api/base44Client";
import { getAppParams } from "@/lib/app-params";

const appId = () => getAppParams().appId;

function authPath(suffix) {
  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{24}$/i.test(appId())) throw new Error("Invalid app ID");
  return `/api/apps/${appId()}/auth/${suffix}`;
}

async function post(suffix, body, token) {
  const response = await fetch(authPath(suffix), {
    method: "POST",
    credentials: "include",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}) },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const result = await response.json();
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error?.message || result.detail || "Unable to sign in");
  return result;
}

export async function loginWithMfa(email, password) {
  const result = await post("login", { email, password });
  if (result.access_token) base44.auth.setToken(result.access_token);
  return result;
}

export async function continueMfa(result, returnTo) {
  const encode = (bytes) => btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes)).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
  const verifier = encode(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)));
  const challenge = encode(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", new TextEncoder().encode(verifier))));
  const { code } = await post("mfa/handoff", { code_challenge: challenge }, result.mfa_session_token);
  sessionStorage.setItem(`app-mfa-pkce:${appId()}`, verifier);
  const url = new URL("/auth/mfa", window.location.origin);
  url.search = new URLSearchParams({ app_id: appId(), mfa_code: code, from_url: returnTo });
  window.location.assign(url.href);
}

// provider: "google" | "microsoft" | "facebook" | "apple"
export async function loginWithSocial(provider, returnTo) {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/apps/public/login-info/by-id/${appId()}`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Unable to load sign-in options");
  const info = await response.json();
  if (info.auth_config?.mfa_policy !== "required") return base44.auth.loginWithProvider(provider, returnTo);
  const url = new URL("/auth/mfa", window.location.origin);
  url.search = new URLSearchParams({ app_id: appId(), oauth_start: provider, from_url: returnTo });
  window.location.assign(url.href);
}

export function loginWithGoogle(returnTo) {
  return loginWithSocial("google", returnTo);
}


===== src/lib/authMfaFlow.js =====
import { base44 } from '@/api/base44Client';
import { getAppParams } from '@/lib/app-params';

const appId = () => getAppParams().appId;

// The app's own MFA: sign-in challenge, enrollment and factor management, against the app's
// /api/apps/<id>/auth/mfa routes. Credentials stay in memory, never in the URL or storage.

const PKCE_KEY = () => `app-mfa-pkce:${appId()}`;
const encode = bytes => btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes)).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');

export class MfaError extends Error {
  constructor(message, status, retryAfter) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.retryAfter = retryAfter;
  }
  // The challenge or grant is gone (expired or used up): sign in again. A 409 is either a concurrent
  // change or the last-method guard; its message says which, so it gets no sign-in link.
  get mustSignInAgain() {
    return this.status === 401;
  }
}

function authPath(suffix) {
  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{24}$/i.test(appId())) throw new Error('Invalid app ID');
  return `/api/apps/${appId()}/auth/${suffix}`;
}

async function request(suffix, { method = 'POST', body, token, headers } = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(authPath(suffix), {
    method,
    credentials: 'include',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}), ...headers },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!response.ok) {
    const message = result.error?.message || result.detail || 'Something went wrong. Try again.';
    throw new MfaError(message, response.status, Number(response.headers.get('Retry-After')) || null);
  }
  return result;
}

// One MFA credential (sign-in challenge, then the management grant) and every call made with it.
// Each success may rotate the grant and revoke older sessions, so both are swapped every time.
export function mfaSession(challenge) {
  let credential = challenge.mfa_session_token;
  const call = async (suffix, body, method) => {
    const result = await request(`mfa/${suffix}`, { method, body, token: credential });
    if (result.mfa_management_token) credential = result.mfa_management_token;
    if (result.access_token) base44.auth.setToken(result.access_token);
    return result;
  };
  return {
    // Sign-in with an enrolled method. method: 'totp' | 'sms'
    verify: (code, method, rememberDevice) => call('verify', { code, method, remember_device: rememberDevice }),
    sendSignInCode: () => call('resend'),
    // Enrollment, and adding or replacing a method during management.
    setupAuthenticator: () => call('totp/setup', {}),
    confirmAuthenticator: (code, rememberDevice = false) => call('totp/verify', { code, remember_device: rememberDevice }),
    sendPhoneCode: phoneNumber => call('sms/send-code', { phone_number: phoneNumber }),
    confirmPhone: (code, rememberDevice = false) => call('sms/verify', { code, remember_device: rememberDevice }),
    // Management only.
    status: () => call('status', undefined, 'GET'),
    sendProofCode: () => call('sms/send-code', { phone_number: '' }),
    removeMethod: (method, code, proofMethod) => call('disable', { method, code, verification_method: proofMethod }),
    forgetDevices: (code, proofMethod) => call('trusted-devices/clear', { code, method: proofMethod }),
  };
}

// Password sign-in that opens factor management instead of signing in.
export function signInToManageMfa(email, password) {
  return request('login', { body: { email, password }, headers: { 'X-App-Mfa-Action': 'manage' } });
}

let resumed;

// Picks up the challenge a social or SSO sign-in sends back to /mfa?mfa_code=…
export function resumeMfa() {
  // The code works once, so a second call (e.g. React StrictMode) reuses the first exchange.
  resumed ||= (async () => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const code = params.get('mfa_code');
    if (!code) return null;
    const verifier = sessionStorage.getItem(PKCE_KEY());
    sessionStorage.removeItem(PKCE_KEY());
    params.delete('mfa_code');
    const rest = params.toString();
    window.history.replaceState(null, '', `${window.location.pathname}${rest ? `?${rest}` : ''}`);
    const result = await request('mfa/exchange', { body: { code, code_verifier: verifier } });
    if (result.access_token) base44.auth.setToken(result.access_token);
    return result;
  })();
  return resumed;
}

const isInAppBrowser = () => /LinkedIn|Twitter/i.test(navigator.userAgent);

// The app's sign-in settings: auth_config holds mfa_policy and the enabled sign-in methods.
export async function loadSignInOptions() {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/apps/public/login-info/by-id/${appId()}`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Unable to load sign-in options');
  return response.json();
}

// provider: 'google' | 'microsoft' | 'facebook' | 'apple' | 'sso'. With manage, the sign-in opens
// factor management. Returns to this app's /mfa page when MFA is needed.
export async function signInWithProvider(provider, returnTo, { manage = false } = {}) {
  const info = await loadSignInOptions();
  // Enterprise SSO sign-in is exempt from Base44 MFA; only managing factors asks for it.
  if (!manage && (info.auth_config?.mfa_policy !== 'required' || provider === 'sso')) {
    return base44.auth.loginWithProvider(provider, returnTo);
  }
  if (isInAppBrowser()) {
    // In-app browsers hand sign-in to the system browser through the platform's MFA page.
    const url = new URL('/auth/mfa', window.location.origin);
    url.search = new URLSearchParams({ app_id: appId(), oauth_start: provider, from_url: returnTo, ...(manage ? { security: '1' } : {}) });
    window.location.assign(url.href);
    return;
  }
  const verifier = encode(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)));
  const challenge = encode(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(verifier))));
  sessionStorage.setItem(PKCE_KEY(), verifier);
  const workspaceSso = provider === 'sso' && info.auth_config?.use_workspace_sso && info.organization_id;
  const path = provider === 'sso'
    ? (workspaceSso ? `/api/apps/${info.organization_id}/${appId()}/auth/sso/login` : `/api/apps/${appId()}/auth/sso/login`)
    : `/api/apps/auth${provider === 'google' ? '' : `/${provider}`}/login`;
  const url = new URL(path, window.location.origin);
  url.search = new URLSearchParams({
    from_url: new URL(returnTo, window.location.origin).href,
    ...(workspaceSso ? {} : { app_id: appId() }),
    ...(manage ? { app_mfa_action: 'manage' } : {}),
    app_mfa_code_challenge: challenge,
    app_mfa_return: '/mfa',
  });
  window.location.assign(url.href);
}

// After MFA: a popup sign-in hands the session to the window that opened it; otherwise go on.
export function finishSignIn(accessToken, returnTo) {
  if (new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('popup') === '1' && window.opener) {
    window.opener.postMessage({ access_token: accessToken }, window.location.origin);
    window.close();
    return;
  }
  window.location.href = returnTo;
}


===== src/lib/authReturnTo.js =====
// Post-auth destination, shared by the auth pages and the MCP consent page.
// Keep the check in one place — it is security-sensitive.
//
// Reads ?returnTo= (our pages) or ?from_url= (what the platform's redirectToLogin
// and login-gate redirects send). A same-origin check alone is not enough: a
// value like "/.//evil.com" or "/\evil.com" parses same-origin but normalizes
// to a protocol-relative "//evil.com" when assigned to location.href, so the
// resolved path must start with exactly one "/" and contain no backslash.
//
// An auth page is never a destination: coming back to /reset-password?token=…
// would carry a live one-time credential through the login round-trip (and,
// via Google, into the provider's OAuth state), and the other three would just
// loop. `/oauth/consent` is a legitimate destination and is not in this set.
const AUTH_PAGES = new Set(["/login", "/register", "/forgot-password", "/reset-password"]);

export function safeReturnTo(search) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return "/";
  const params = new URLSearchParams(search ?? window.location.search);
  return sanitizeReturnTo(params.get("returnTo") || params.get("from_url"));
}

export function sanitizeReturnTo(raw) {
  if (!raw || typeof window === "undefined") return "/";
  try {
    const url = new URL(raw, window.location.origin);
    if (url.origin !== window.location.origin) return "/";
    // Bootstrap params must never ride through a login round-trip and get
    // persisted into the fresh session. App-flow params (e.g. the consent ctx) stay.
    for (const p of ["access_token", "clear_access_token", "app_id", "app_base_url", "functions_version", "from_url", "returnTo"]) {
      url.searchParams.delete(p);
    }
    if (AUTH_PAGES.has(url.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, "") || "/")) return "/";
    const path = url.pathname + url.search;
    if (!path.startsWith("/") || path.startsWith("//") || path.includes("\\")) return "/";
    return path;
  } catch {
    return "/";
  }
}

// The current page as a returnTo value: same rules as any other destination, so
// an auth page or a bootstrap param never becomes the way back.
export function currentPath() {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return "/";
  return sanitizeReturnTo(window.location.pathname + window.location.search);
}

// The current page as the absolute URL the platform login's from_url expects.
export function currentUrl() {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return "/";
  return window.location.origin + currentPath();
}

// `search` for a <Link> to an auth page that should come back to `returnTo`.
export function returnToSearch(returnTo) {
  return returnTo && returnTo !== "/" ? { returnTo } : {};
}


===== src/lib/codigo-fns.js =====
import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { z } from "zod";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/auth-middleware";

// Só a equipe Payonda pode levar o código do app embora.
async function lerFontes(context) {
  if (context.user?.role !== "admin") throw new Error("acesso_restrito");
  const { ARQUIVOS } = await import("@/lib/codigo-fonte.server.js");
  return ARQUIVOS;
}

// Lista enxuta: só caminhos e tamanhos, para a página abrir rápido.
export const listarArquivos = createServerFn({ method: "GET" })
  .middleware([requireUser])
  .handler(async ({ context }) => {
    const arquivos = await lerFontes(context);
    return arquivos.map((arquivo) => ({
      caminho: arquivo.caminho,
      tamanho: arquivo.conteudo.length,
    }));
  });

export const lerArquivo = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .middleware([requireUser])
  .validator(z.object({ caminho: z.string().min(1).max(200) }))
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => {
    const arquivos = await lerFontes(context);
    const achado = arquivos.find((arquivo) => arquivo.caminho === data.caminho);
    if (!achado) throw new Error("arquivo_nao_encontrado");
    return achado;
  });

export const lerLote = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .middleware([requireUser])
  .validator(z.object({ caminhos: z.array(z.string().min(1).max(200)).min(1).max(20) }))
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => {
    const arquivos = await lerFontes(context);
    const conteudos = new Map(arquivos.map((arquivo) => [arquivo.caminho, arquivo.conteudo]));
    return data.caminhos.map((caminho) => ({
      caminho,
      conteudo: conteudos.get(caminho) ?? "",
    }));
  });

===== src/lib/error-capture.js =====
// Base44 managed file: error-capture v1. Keep it as it is, and keep both middlewares first in src/start.js;
// Base44 replaces it with newer versions. Each reports the error to Base44 and rethrows it, and a 5xx response the
// app returned goes out as it is, so what a visitor gets back is unchanged.
import { isNotFound, isRedirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { createMiddleware } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { recordErrorResponse, recordFunctionError, recordRequestError } from "@/lib/error-capture.server";

// Control flow, not failures: TanStack turns these into redirects, not-found pages and the thrown response.
const isControlFlow = (error) => error instanceof Response || isRedirect(error) || isNotFound(error);

// A server function's error, before TanStack serializes it for the caller.
export const errorCaptureFunctionMiddleware = createMiddleware({ type: "function" }).server(
  async ({ next, serverFnMeta }) => {
    try {
      return await next();
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isControlFlow(error)) recordFunctionError(error, serverFnMeta);
      throw error;
    }
  },
);

// An error that escapes a server route, another middleware or the page's rendering, and a 5xx one of them returns.
// A server function call's own errors come from the function middleware; what else escapes one is TanStack's (an
// unknown function id).
export const errorCaptureRequestMiddleware = createMiddleware({ type: "request" }).server(
  async ({ next, request, handlerType }) => {
    let result;
    try {
      result = await next();
    } catch (error) {
      if (handlerType !== "serverFn" && !isControlFlow(error)) recordRequestError(request, error);
      throw error;
    }
    if (handlerType !== "serverFn") recordErrorResponse(request, result);
    return result;
  },
);


===== src/lib/error-capture.server.js =====
// Base44 managed file: error-capture v1. Keep it as it is; Base44 replaces it with newer versions.
// It reports the app's server errors to Base44, so the app's owner sees them as alerts.
import { getRequest, getResponseStatus } from "@tanstack/react-start/server";
import { env, waitUntil } from "cloudflare:workers";

// Base44 sends it only on production requests of apps whose owner turned error reporting on.
const FEED_CONTEXT_HEADER = "x-b44-feed-context";
const SECRET_MIN_CHARS = 8;
const NAME_MAX_CHARS = 64;
const MESSAGE_MAX_BYTES = 500;
const STACK_MAX_BYTES = 4 * 1024;
const LOGGED_ERRORS_KEPT = 8;
const BODY_MAX_BYTES = 2 * 1024;
const BODY_WAIT_MS = 50;

const encoder = new TextEncoder();
const decoder = new TextDecoder();
// The errors each request recorded. One error can surface twice: a server function's error also fails the loader
// that called it. Per request, since requests can share an Error, such as a rejected init promise. A request with a
// record gets none for the 5xx it then answers.
const recorded = new WeakMap();
// The Errors each stamped request logged, newest last.
const loggedErrors = new WeakMap();

function cutBytes(text, maxBytes) {
  const bytes = encoder.encode(text);
  if (bytes.length <= maxBytes) return text;
  let end = maxBytes;
  while (end > 0 && (bytes[end] & 0xc0) === 0x80) end--;
  return decoder.decode(bytes.subarray(0, end));
}

function cutStack(stack) {
  const cut = cutBytes(stack, STACK_MAX_BYTES);
  const lineEnd = cut === stack ? -1 : cut.lastIndexOf("\n");
  return lineEnd > 0 ? cut.slice(0, lineEnd) : cut;
}

function scrubber() {
  const secrets = Object.values(env)
    .filter((value) => typeof value === "string" && value.length >= SECRET_MIN_CHARS)
    .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
  return (text) => secrets.reduce((scrubbed, secret) => scrubbed.replaceAll(secret, "[redacted]"), text.toWellFormed());
}

// One `_b44_error` record for Base44's error tail. A 4xx is the caller's, and an error without a message says
// nothing: neither is recorded.
function write(request, error, { status, fn, route }) {
  if (status < 500 || !request.headers.has(FEED_CONTEXT_HEADER) || request.signal.aborted) return;
  const seen = recorded.get(request);
  if (seen?.has(error)) return;
  const raw = typeof error === "string" ? error : error?.message;
  if (typeof raw !== "string" || !raw.trim()) return;
  const scrub = scrubber();
  const record = {
    v: 1,
    kind: "response",
    status,
    name: typeof error?.name === "string" ? Array.from(scrub(error.name)).slice(0, NAME_MAX_CHARS).join("") : undefined,
    message: cutBytes(scrub(raw), MESSAGE_MAX_BYTES),
    stack: typeof error?.stack === "string" ? cutStack(scrub(error.stack)) : undefined,
    fn,
    route,
  };
  const line = { _b44_env: "prod", level: "error", message: JSON.stringify({ _b44_error: record }) };
  recorded.set(request, (seen ?? new Set()).add(error));
  console.log(line);
}

// Reporting never changes the request: any failure writes nothing.
function safely(report) {
  try {
    report();
  } catch {
    // Nothing to do.
  }
}

function stampedRequest() {
  try {
    const request = getRequest();
    return request.headers.has(FEED_CONTEXT_HEADER) ? request : undefined;
  } catch {
    return undefined; // Not inside a request.
  }
}

function remember(args) {
  const request = stampedRequest();
  if (!request) return;
  const errors = args.filter((arg) => arg instanceof Error);
  if (errors.length === 0) return;
  loggedErrors.set(request, [...(loggedErrors.get(request) ?? []), ...errors].slice(-LOGGED_ERRORS_KEPT));
}

// A 5xx the app answers itself is described from the Errors it logged.
for (const method of ["debug", "dir", "error", "info", "log", "trace", "warn"]) {
  const original = console[method];
  console[method] = (...args) => {
    safely(() => remember(args));
    return original.apply(console, args);
  };
}

// The body as JSON, if it is at most BODY_MAX_BYTES and arrives within BODY_WAIT_MS.
async function readJson(response) {
  const reader = response.body?.getReader();
  if (!reader) return undefined;
  let timer;
  const late = new Promise((resolve) => (timer = setTimeout(resolve, BODY_WAIT_MS)));
  try {
    const chunks = [];
    let size = 0;
    for (;;) {
      const read = await Promise.race([reader.read(), late]);
      if (!read) return undefined;
      if (read.done) return JSON.parse(await new Blob(chunks).text());
      size += read.value.byteLength;
      if (size > BODY_MAX_BYTES) return undefined;
      chunks.push(read.value);
    }
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    reader.cancel().catch(() => {});
  }
}

const strings = (value) =>
  typeof value === "string" ? [value] : value && typeof value === "object" ? Object.values(value).flatMap(strings) : [];

// A generic `error` gives way to the `details` beside it.
function bodyMessage(body) {
  if (!body || typeof body !== "object") return undefined;
  const generic = body.error === "Internal server error" && "details" in body;
  const fields = [generic ? undefined : body.error, body.message, body.detail, body.details, body.error?.message];
  return fields.find((value) => typeof value === "string" && value.trim());
}

// A logged Error the body repeats, else the body's own message, else the last logged Error.
function describe(body, errors) {
  const texts = strings(body);
  const repeated = errors.findLast(
    ({ message }) => typeof message === "string" && message.trim() && texts.some((text) => text.includes(message)),
  );
  return repeated ?? bodyMessage(body) ?? errors.at(-1);
}

// TanStack answers a server function's error with the status the code set, else 200.
export const recordFunctionError = (error, serverFnMeta) =>
  safely(() => {
    const status = getResponseStatus();
    write(getRequest(), error, { status: status >= 400 ? status : 500, fn: serverFnMeta?.name ?? serverFnMeta?.id });
  });

// An escaped error answers with the status it carries, as TanStack's server reads it, else 500.
export const recordRequestError = (request, error) =>
  safely(() => {
    const status = error?.status ?? error?.statusCode;
    write(request, error, { status: Number.isInteger(status) && status >= 400 && status <= 599 ? status : 500 });
  });

// An errored route match renders its error component with a 500.
export const recordRouteError = (request, error, route) => safely(() => write(request, error, { status: 500, route }));

// A 5xx the app returned itself, such as a server route's caught error. The response goes out as it is; a copy of
// it is read after that.
export const recordErrorResponse = (request, result) =>
  safely(() => {
    const status = result?.response?.status;
    if (!(status >= 500) || !request.headers.has(FEED_CONTEXT_HEADER) || recorded.has(request)) return;
    const copy = result.response.clone();
    waitUntil(
      readJson(copy)
        .then((body) => {
          if (!recorded.has(request)) write(request, describe(body, loggedErrors.get(request) ?? []), { status });
        })
        .catch(() => {}),
    );
  });


===== src/lib/filtro-admin.js =====
import { ETAPAS, MEIO_LABEL, SEGMENTO_LABEL } from "@/lib/app-data";

const ETAPA_LABEL = Object.fromEntries(ETAPAS.map((etapa) => [etapa.id, etapa.label]));

const digitos = (valor) => String(valor ?? "").replace(/\D/g, "");

function casaToken(token, campos, valores) {
  const texto = campos.filter(Boolean).join(" ").toLowerCase();
  if (texto.includes(token)) return true;

  const alvo = digitos(token);
  if (alvo.length < 2) return false;

  return valores.some((valor) => {
    if (!Number.isFinite(valor)) return false;
    return digitos(valor.toFixed(2).replace(".", ",")).includes(alvo);
  });
}

function casaTermo(termo, campos, valores) {
  const tokens = termo
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean);
  if (tokens.length === 0) return true;
  return tokens.every((token) => casaToken(token, campos, valores));
}

export function filtrarEmpresas(empresas, termo) {
  return empresas.filter((empresa) =>
    casaTermo(
      termo,
      [
        empresa.nome,
        empresa.cnpj,
        empresa.contato,
        empresa.notas,
        SEGMENTO_LABEL[empresa.segmento] ?? empresa.segmento,
        ETAPA_LABEL[empresa.etapa] ?? empresa.etapa,
      ],
      [empresa.volume_mensal],
    ),
  );
}

export function filtrarLancamentos(lancamentos, termo) {
  return lancamentos.filter((lancamento) =>
    casaTermo(
      termo,
      [
        lancamento.descricao,
        MEIO_LABEL[lancamento.meio] ?? lancamento.meio,
        lancamento.status,
        lancamento.natureza,
      ],
      [lancamento.valor],
    ),
  );
}

===== src/lib/fluxo-fns.js =====
import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/auth-middleware";
import { mascararChave } from "@/lib/link-pagamento";

const LIMITE = 120;

/**
 * Trilha do dinheiro: cada cobrança gerada por um cliente, a tarifa retida pela
 * Payonda, o líquido creditado e a chave de destino. Só a equipe Payonda lê.
 */
export const fluxoDoDinheiro = createServerFn({ method: "GET" })
  .middleware([requireUser])
  .handler(async ({ context }) => {
    if (context.user.role !== "admin") {
      throw Object.assign(new Error("Forbidden"), { status: 403 });
    }

    const sdk = context.getBase44();
    const admin = sdk.asServiceRole;

    const [pagina, usuarios, pagos, ativas] = await Promise.all([
      sdk.entities.LinkPagamento.list({ sort: "-created_date", limit: LIMITE }),
      admin.entities.User.list(),
      admin.entities.LinkPagamento.aggregate({
        query: { status: "pago" },
        sum: ["valor", "taxa", "valor_liquido"],
      }),
      sdk.entities.LinkPagamento.count({ status: "ativo" }),
    ]);

    const nomes = new Map((usuarios ?? []).map((u) => [u.id, u.full_name || u.email || u.id]));

    const itens = (pagina?.items ?? pagina ?? []).map((link) => ({
      id: link.id,
      codigo: link.codigo,
      titulo: link.titulo,
      valor: Number(link.valor ?? 0),
      taxa: Number(link.taxa ?? 0),
      liquido: Number(link.valor_liquido ?? 0),
      status: link.status,
      recebedor: link.recebedor ?? "",
      chave: link.chave_pix ? mascararChave(link.chave_pix) : "—",
      cliente: nomes.get(link.created_by_id) ?? "conta removida",
      criado_em: link.created_date ?? null,
      pago_em: link.pago_em ?? null,
    }));

    const soma = pagos?.rows?.[0] ?? {};
    const resumo = {
      pagas: Number(soma.count ?? 0),
      ativas: Number(ativas ?? 0),
      volume: Number(soma.sum_valor ?? 0),
      tarifa: Number(soma.sum_taxa ?? 0),
      liquido: Number(soma.sum_valor_liquido ?? 0),
    };

    return { itens, resumo };
  });

===== src/lib/link-fns.js =====
import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { z } from "zod";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/auth-middleware";
import {
  ambientePix,
  gerarBrCode,
  gerarTxid,
  pspConsultarCobranca,
  pspCriarCobranca,
} from "@/lib/pix.server";
import { gerarCodigo, mascararChave, tarifasDoMeio } from "@/lib/link-pagamento";

const VALIDADE_DIAS = 7;

const arredondar = (valor) => Math.round(Number(valor) * 100) / 100;

async function porCodigo(sdk, codigo) {
  const pagina = await sdk.entities.LinkPagamento.filter({ codigo }, { limit: 1 });
  return (pagina?.items ?? pagina ?? [])[0] ?? null;
}

const EntradaLink = z.object({
  titulo: z.string().min(2).max(120),
  valor: z.number().positive().max(5_000_000),
  descricao: z.string().max(200).optional(),
  pagador_nome: z.string().max(120).optional(),
  chave_pix: z.string().min(5).max(77),
  recebedor: z.string().max(60).optional(),
});

/** Cria o link já com o BR Code do Pix pronto, montado no padrão do Bacen. */
export const criarLink = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .middleware([requireUser])
  .validator(EntradaLink)
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => {
    const sdk = context.getBase44();
    const valor = arredondar(data.valor);
    const txid = gerarTxid();
    const recebedor = (data.recebedor ?? "").trim() || context.user.full_name || "PAYONDA";
    const expira = new Date(Date.now() + VALIDADE_DIAS * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const tarifas = tarifasDoMeio(valor);

    const brcode = gerarBrCode({
      chave: data.chave_pix,
      nome: recebedor,
      valor,
      txid,
      descricao: data.titulo,
    });

    const link = await sdk.entities.LinkPagamento.create({
      codigo: gerarCodigo(),
      titulo: data.titulo.trim(),
      descricao: data.descricao?.trim() ?? "",
      valor,
      meio: "pix",
      status: "ativo",
      pagador_nome: data.pagador_nome?.trim() ?? "",
      expira_em: expira,
      pagamento_id: txid,
      pagamento_expira_em: `${expira}T23:59:59`,
      pix_copia_e_cola: brcode,
      chave_pix: data.chave_pix.trim(),
      recebedor,
      taxa: tarifas.taxa,
      taxa_mercado: tarifas.taxa_mercado,
      valor_liquido: tarifas.valor_liquido,
    });

    // Com PSP configurado a cobrança também é registrada na API Pix do banco.
    if (ambientePix() === "banco") {
      await pspCriarCobranca({
        txid,
        chave: data.chave_pix.trim(),
        valor,
        descricao: data.titulo,
      }).catch(() => null);
    }

    return link;
  });

export const listarLinks = createServerFn({ method: "GET" })
  .middleware([requireUser])
  .handler(async ({ context }) => {
    const pagina = await context
      .getBase44()
      .entities.LinkPagamento.filter(
        { created_by_id: context.user.id },
        { sort: "-created_date", limit: 40 },
      );
    return pagina?.items ?? [];
  });

/** Leitura pública: só o que o pagador precisa ver. */
export const abrirLink = createServerFn({ method: "GET" })
  .validator(z.object({ codigo: z.string().min(3).max(20) }))
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => {
    const link = await porCodigo(context.getBase44().asServiceRole, data.codigo);
    if (!link) return null;

    const tarifas = tarifasDoMeio(link.valor);

    return {
      codigo: link.codigo,
      titulo: link.titulo,
      descricao: link.descricao ?? "",
      valor: link.valor,
      taxa: Number(link.taxa) > 0 ? link.taxa : tarifas.taxa,
      taxa_mercado: Number(link.taxa_mercado) > 0 ? link.taxa_mercado : tarifas.taxa_mercado,
      valor_liquido:
        Number(link.valor_liquido) > 0 ? link.valor_liquido : tarifas.valor_liquido,
      status: link.status,
      expira_em: link.expira_em ?? null,
      pago_em: link.pago_em ?? null,
      pagador_nome: link.pagador_nome ?? "",
      recebedor: link.recebedor ?? "",
      pix_copia_e_cola: link.pix_copia_e_cola ?? "",
      chave_mascarada: mascararChave(link.chave_pix),
      ambiente: ambientePix(),
    };
  });

/**
 * Baixa do Pix: só com CONCLUIDA vinda da API Pix do banco. Sem PSP conectado
 * não existe confirmação possível, então o link continua aguardando pagamento:
 * nenhuma ação do pagador (ou do navegador) marca a cobrança como paga.
 */
export const verificarPixPago = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .validator(z.object({ codigo: z.string().min(3).max(24) }))
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => {
    const sdk = context.getBase44().asServiceRole;
    const link = await porCodigo(sdk, data.codigo);
    if (!link) return { status: "erro" };
    if (link.status === "pago") return { status: "pago", pago_em: link.pago_em };
    if (link.status !== "ativo") return { status: "erro" };
    if (!link.pagamento_id) return { status: "erro" };

    // Sem credenciais do PSP não há verificação bancária: não concede baixa.
    if (ambientePix() !== "banco") return { status: "indisponivel" };

    const cobranca = await pspConsultarCobranca(link.pagamento_id).catch(() => null);
    if (cobranca?.status !== "CONCLUIDA") return { status: "aguardando" };

    const valor = Number(link.valor ?? 0);
    const tarifas = tarifasDoMeio(valor);
    const pago_em = new Date().toISOString();
    const pagador = cobranca?.pix?.[0]?.pagador?.nome ?? link.pagador_nome ?? "";

    await sdk.entities.LinkPagamento.update(link.id, {
      status: "pago",
      pago_em,
      meio_pago: "pix",
      pagador_nome: pagador,
      taxa: tarifas.taxa,
      taxa_mercado: tarifas.taxa_mercado,
      valor_liquido: tarifas.valor_liquido,
    });

    await sdk.entities.Lancamento.create({
      descricao: `Link ${link.codigo} · ${link.titulo}`,
      data: pago_em.slice(0, 10),
      meio: "pix",
      natureza: "entrada",
      valor,
      taxa: tarifas.taxa,
      taxa_mercado: tarifas.taxa_mercado,
      status: "aprovado",
    });

    return { status: "pago", pago_em };
  });

===== src/lib/link-pagamento.js =====
import { taxasPix } from "@/lib/pix-taxas";

/** Meios previstos no link. Só o Pix está liberado hoje. */
export const MEIOS_LINK = [
  { id: "pix", label: "Pix", ativo: true },
  { id: "boleto", label: "Boleto", ativo: false },
  { id: "cartao", label: "Cartão", ativo: false },
];

const ALFABETO_CODIGO = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789"; // 32 símbolos

/** Código público com 10 símbolos aleatórios de fonte criptográfica (32^10 combinações). */
export function gerarCodigo() {
  const bytes = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(10));
  let saida = "";
  for (const byte of bytes) saida += ALFABETO_CODIGO[byte % ALFABETO_CODIGO.length];
  return `PY-${saida}`;
}

/** A chave recebedora não circula inteira na página pública. */
export function mascararChave(chave) {
  const texto = String(chave ?? "");
  const arroba = texto.indexOf("@");
  if (arroba > 0) return `${texto.slice(0, 2)}***${texto.slice(arroba)}`;
  if (texto.length <= 4) return texto;
  return `***${texto.slice(-4)}`;
}

export const tarifasDoMeio = (valor) => taxasPix(valor);
